Result: 理想合成-合成条件下，Ω匹配预训练可超越oracle基线（MSE降低+46.8%，LSTM），但收益已依赖架构。ETTm1上关系不成立：正弦加噪源在三架构平均仅+2.3%（单通道最高+65.5%），而Ω匹配的混沌ODE源系统性变差（-13.2%）。两类源在仅53%配置上迁移方向一致（Pearson r=-0.02），说明Ω几乎不携带源迁移有益性的信息。还观察到对Ω差距方向的架构相关不对称响应。

Conclusion: Ω匹配在受控情形下有效，但不足以预测跨源迁移；源选择准则需考虑额外信号属性。

Abstract: Synthetic pretraining under distribution shift requires deciding what synthetic data to use. A natural criterion is to match the spectral predictability $Ω$ of the source to the target, but is $Ω$ sufficient? We test this hypothesis, which we call predictability-aware pretraining, across three architectures (LSTM, PatchTST, TCN) and three seeds. Under ideal synthetic-to-synthetic conditions, $Ω$-matched pretraining can outperform even an oracle baseline (+46.8% MSE reduction, LSTM), though the benefit is already architecture-dependent. On the real-world ETTm1 benchmark, however, this relationship does not hold: sine-plus-noise pretraining at matched $Ω$ yields only a marginal mean improvement +2.3% averaged over all three architectures, up to +65.5% on individual channels), whereas $Ω$ matched chaotic ODE trajectories systematically degrade performance -13.2%). The two sources agree on the direction of transfer for only 53% of configurations across all three architectures (Pearson r=-0.02), indicating that $Ω$ carries little information about which source will transfer beneficially. We further observe an architecture-dependent asymmetry in the response to the direction of the $Ω$ gap. We conclude that $Ω$ matching can help in controlled settings but is not sufficient to predict transfer across sources, and we discuss additional signal properties that a source-selection criterion may need to consider.

</details>


### [174] [Neural Spectral Capacity with Residual Context for Heterogeneous Resource Allocation within Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30823)
*Chenyu Zhu,Ruoyu Zhao,Zhichao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出残差感知的神经谱容量ResNSC及分配器ResNSC-DP，在固定预算下联合分配残差与FFN宽度，优于多种基线分配与NSC剪枝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练成本高，需要仅凭结构规格预测相对训练性能以指导设计；已有NSC独立评分各投影，且对同一层配置的兼容重排给出相同分数，忽略残差累积位置效应。

Method: 为Transformer构造由规格导出的残差累积更新统计参考，确定每个投影容量计算中的输入与残差状态统计，保持无需数据与实例化权重的可加性评估；将NSC-DP的状态从"层-预算"扩展为含残差历史，联合分配残差宽度与前馈宽度，并对有限候选集内最优数值评估ResNSC分数给出认证边界。

Result: 121M与374M预训练规模下，在FineWeb-Edu、WikiText-2、LAMBADA上、同等参数预算下困惑度低于均匀、变宽与锥形分配；LoNAS超网继承权重的LLaMA-7B剪枝中，八个匹配预算下八任务平均精度较NSC提升3.28–5.46个百分点。

Conclusion: 残差历史感知的位置相关评分能揭示被位置无关评分掩盖的性能差异；配对互换实验保持投影形状与NSC分数不变却使性能下降，验证该效应。

Abstract: Predicting relative trained performance from architectural specifications can guide design when training costs limit experimentation. Neural Spectral Capacity (NSC) provides such a heuristic, but scores projections independently and assigns identical scores to compatible reorderings of the same layer configurations. We introduce Residual-Aware Neural Spectral Capacity (ResNSC) for Transformers, using a specification-derived statistical reference for accumulated residual updates to determine the input and residual-state statistics in each projection's capacity calculation, while retaining additive evaluation without data or instantiated weights. When residual widths vary, earlier choices affect later scores, so we augment NSC's allocator (NSC-DP) with residual history beyond its layer-and-budget state. ResNSC-DP jointly allocates residual and feed-forward widths under a resource budget, with certified bounds on the best numerically evaluated ResNSC score within a specified finite candidate set. At the 121M and 374M pretraining scales, selected architectures achieve lower mean perplexity than uniform, variable-width, and tapered allocations on FineWeb-Edu, WikiText-2, and LAMBADA at comparable parameter budgets. In inherited-weight LLaMA-7B pruning using the LoNAS supernet, ResNSC-DP improves eight-task average accuracy over NSC by 3.28--5.46 percentage points across eight matched parameter budgets. Paired reversals preserve the projection shapes and NSC scores yet worsen performance in both settings, exposing performance differences hidden by position-independent scoring. The code for this work is available at https://github.com/Optima-CityU/neural-spectral-capacity.

</details>


### [175] [Reverse-KL Policy Distillation: Statistical Guarantees and Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30844)
*Haolin Liu,Chen-Yu Wei,Julian Zimmert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为 reverse-KL 蒸馏建立统计理论，研究 online 与 offline 两种设定在"教师提供完整动作分布"与"仅提供采样动作概率"两种反馈模型下的样本复杂度，给出 minimax 界并指出 online 与 offline 的本质统计差异，同时提出实现最优界的技术手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Reverse-KL 蒸馏在语言模型训练中已有实证效果，但其统计保证与理论极限不清。尤其两个设计选择的影响未知：(1) 获取教师完整动作分布是否优于仅观测其对采样动作的概率；(2) 训练轨迹必须来自学生（online）还是可用教师生成轨迹（offline）。作者希望为这些问题提供理论起点。

Method: 在两种反馈模型（教师揭示完整动作分布 vs. 仅揭示学生生成动作的概率）下研究 online reverse-KL 蒸馏的样本复杂度：为前者建立 minimax 界，并证明后者可能需要显著更多数据；同时分析 offline reverse-KL 蒸馏在完整分布与仅教师生成动作概率两种情形下均可达到相同 minimax 速率。作者进一步用下界说明常见 online 目标函数在候选仅基于历史学生轨迹评估时会失效，并提出 trajectory-loss、importance-ratio truncation 结合 policy-space learning 的技术来达到最优 online 保证。

Result: Online 设定下，完整分布反馈具有更优 minimax 界，仅概率反馈可能需要显著更多样本；Offline 设定下，两种反馈模型均达到相同 minimax 速率。关键在于教师生成动作本身揭示了教师偏好，而在学生选择的动作上查询可能使偏好未被观测到。下界表明标准 online 目标函数可能失败。

Conclusion: 教师生成动作本身携带偏好信息，是 offline 蒸馏统计上不逊于 online 的核心原因；online 蒸馏因在 student-selected actions 上查询而面临额外统计挑战。这些保证无法仅靠优化标准蒸馏目标获得，需借助 trajectory-loss、importance-ratio truncation 与 policy-space learning 才能实现最优 online 蒸馏。

Abstract: Reverse Kullback-Leibler (KL) distillation has shown empirical promise for training language models, but its statistical guarantees and limits remain poorly understood. In particular, the effects of two design choices remain unclear: Does access to the teacher's full action distribution provide an advantage over observing only the probability it assigns to the sampled action? Must training trajectories come from the student (online distillation), or can teacher-generated trajectories (offline distillation) suffice? Our work takes a first step toward understanding the theory underlying these questions.
  We study the sample complexity of online reverse-KL distillation under two feedback models: the teacher reveals either its full action distribution or only the probability of the student-generated action. We establish minimax bounds for the former and show that substantially more data may be required for the latter. In contrast, offline reverse-KL distillation can attain the same minimax rate whether the teacher reveals its full distribution or only its probability of the teacher-generated action. The key distinction is that teacher-generated actions themselves reveal the teacher's preferences, while queries at student-selected actions can leave those preferences unobserved. This highlights an additional statistical challenge for online distillation compared to offline distillation.
  These statistical guarantees do not follow simply from optimizing standard distillation objectives: our lower bounds show that common online objectives can fail when candidates are evaluated only on past student-generated trajectories. We develop trajectory-loss and importance-ratio truncation techniques together with policy-space learning to achieve optimal online distillation guarantees.

</details>


### [176] [Bifurcations from Weight Space: JEPAWG on Parametric Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30847)
*Tobias Göbel,Miranda C. N. Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文用四个动力系统构建"模型动物园"作为基准，检验联合嵌入超网络JEPAWG能否从训练网络权重群体中读出物理结构：它在预测任务上具竞争力，并能定位Hopf点、周期加倍级联（可定性估计Feigenbaum常数）及无特征值信号的全局分岔。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络权重日益被当作数据处理，但"一族模型应编码什么"缺乏真值，使权重空间方法难以系统验证。参数化动力系统提供了理想测试床：其相空间定性变化发生在参数空间的子流形上，且这些分岔在成熟系统族中可由解析或数值确定，从而为权重空间诊断提供可核对的标准答案。

Method: 在参数网格的每一点上微调预测网络，得到以物理参数为索引的模型动物园（四个动力系统）；再用JEPAWG——一种围绕学习到的参数—权重表示构建的联合嵌入超网络——进行权重空间分析，检验解码权重的参数导数范数等诊断量是否与已知分岔结构对应。

Result: JEPAWG在预测性能上与直接为该任务训练的超网络相当，尽管其监督信号是参数—权重对应关系而非预测。解码权重对参数导数之范数可定位Hopf点与Hopf曲线，紧密追踪周期加倍级联并给出Feigenbaum常数的定性估计，还能检测无特征值特征、对线性化不可见的全局分岔。

Conclusion: JEPAWG提供了其他超网络无法给出的可解释动力学洞见，表明它是从训练网络群体中读取物理结构的可行工具。

Abstract: Trained network weights are increasingly treated as data, but there is rarely ground truth about what a population of models should encode, which hampers systematic validation of weight-space methods. This makes parametric dynamical systems a natural test-bed: the dynamics change qualitatively at submanifolds of parameter space that are, in well-studied families, known analytically or numerically. Fine-tuning a forecasting network at each point of a parameter grid yields a model zoo indexed by a physical parameter, against which weight-space diagnostics can be checked.
  We build such model zoos for four dynamical systems and analyse them with JEPAWG, a recently proposed joint-embedding hypernetwork built around a learned parameter--weight representation. It was first introduced for neural samplers for lattice field theories, where its latent geometry located the phase transition and estimated the 2D Ising critical exponent. Applied to dynamical systems, JEPAWG is competitive on forecasting with a hypernetwork trained directly for that task, despite being supervised on the parameter--weight correspondence rather than on forecasts. Beyond forecasting, the norm of the parameter-derivative of the decoded weights locates Hopf points and curves, tracks a period-doubling cascade closely enough to give a qualitative estimate of the Feigenbaum constant, and detects a global bifurcation, which has no eigenvalue signature and is therefore invisible to linearisation. As such, JEPAWG provides interpretable insight into the dynamics that is not available from other hypernetworks. Together, these results indicate that JEPAWG is a workable instrument for reading physical structure off a population of trained networks.

</details>


### [177] [Training Mixed-Monotone Neural Networks for Verification Without Set Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30853)
*Jonas Fingerhut,Lukas Koller,Matthias Althoff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种将混合单调函数实现为单调子网络之差的新神经网络架构，使紧输出包络的计算简化为两次前向传播，并可在训练中直接最小化包络尺寸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络形式化验证通常需保守地包住输出集合，但紧输出包络的计算一般计算困难，因此需要绕过该困难。

Method: 利用混合单调系统理论，将网络设计为单调子网络之差；据此仅需对输入区间两个对角端点各做一次前向传播即可得到紧包络，且包络尺寸是权重的闭式函数，可在训练中直接最小化；并给出基于多输入推广的全变差的下界。

Result: 在回归与动力系统基准上，训练将包络尺寸缩小到下界的0.5%以内且不牺牲预测精度；包络计算比CROWN与α-CROWN快数个数量级，在深层网络上比zonotope传播和CROWN更紧，尺寸仅比α-CROWN大5.1%。

Conclusion: 该架构以极低计算代价获得接近最优的紧输出包络，兼顾可验证性与预测精度。

Abstract: Most formal verification approaches for neural networks conservatively enclose the output set of the neural network. However, computing a tight output enclosure is in general computationally hard. We sidestep this challenge with a novel neural network architecture that realizes mixed-monotone functions as the difference of monotone subnetworks. By utilizing the theory on mixed-monotone systems, this reduces the computation of a tight output enclosure from complicated set propagation to two simple forward passes of opposite corners of an input interval. Moreover, because the size of the resulting enclosure is a closed-form function of the weights of the neural network, we can directly minimize it during training. We prove a lower bound on the size of any such enclosure, given by the total variation of the represented function generalized to several inputs, which makes it possible to compare the tightness of the enclosure against the optimum. Our evaluation on regression and dynamical-system benchmarks demonstrates that our training reduces the enclosure size to within 0.5% of the lower bound without sacrificing prediction accuracy. Computing our enclosure is orders of magnitude faster than CROWN and $α$-CROWN and on deep networks it is also tighter than zonotope propagation and CROWN, while its size exceeds that of $α$-CROWN by only 5.1%.

</details>


### [178] [Joules-to-GDP: How Much Economic Value Do AI Systems Produce per Unit of Energy?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30858)
*Amirreza Zeinali,Jon Saad-Falcon,Avanika Narayan,Mersad Abbasi,Jonathan S. Hartley,John Hennessy,Christopher Re,Azalia Mirhoseini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"每焦耳净价值(NVJ)"与"每焦耳节省时间(TSJ)"两个指标，在220个GDPval任务上量化AI系统单位能耗所创造的经济价值，发现任务成功率/美元价值/省时最高的职业并不等于能效回报最高，且模型、加速器与部署规模显著影响能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统产出更具经济价值的工作，但其单位能耗所带来的价值并不明确；现有评估多关注任务成功率或产出质量，缺乏将"经济价值"与"端到端能耗"统一度量的框架，难以判断AI对职业工作的经济效应究竟有多大。

Method: 将经济价值形式化为两类：(1)净美元价值=产出美元价值−AI执行成本；(2)节省时间=专家完成时间−AI完成时间。两者分别除以实测端到端能耗，得到NVJ(USD/MJ)与TSJ(hours/MJ)。实验覆盖44个职业的220个公开GDPval任务，本地部署5个开放权重模型，评测4种加速器类型，并比较不同部署规模（1块 vs 8块H100）。

Result: (1)任务成功率、美元价值或省时最高的职业组未必能效回报最高：工程类每任务净美元价值比软件开发高约10%，但软件开发NVJ高约27%。(2)GLM 5.2平均净美元价值最高，为$72.27/任务、耗能0.430 MJ(约等于4小时笔记本使用)；Gemma 4 31B仅用23%能耗即达其56%净价值，NVJ约2.5倍、TSJ约2.8倍。(3)四种单加速器部署中Qwen3.6-27B的NVJ差异达4.32倍、TSJ差异达9.27倍，B200均领先；1块与8块H100净美元价值相近，但8块将完成时间缩短22%、能耗为3.24倍。

Conclusion: 该框架把"产出价值"与"专家时间节省"在能耗预算下明确为可优化目标，为改进模型、加速器与软件提供依据，并为评估AI对职业工作的潜在经济影响提供新基准；能效视角应作为模型与硬件选型、部署配置的核心决策依据。

Abstract: An AI system can produce more economically valuable work while delivering less value for the energy it consumes. We therefore ask: How much economic value does an AI system create per unit of energy consumed? We formalize economic value in two ways: (1) net dollar value, the output's dollar value minus AI-system execution cost, and (2) time saving, the expert's completion time minus the AI system's completion time. Dividing each by measured end-to-end energy yields Net Value per Joule (NVJ) and Time Saving per Joule (TSJ), reported in USD/MJ and hours/MJ. We study 220 public GDPval tasks across 44 occupations, serving five open-weight models locally and evaluating four accelerator types. First, the occupation groups where AI systems succeed most often, deliver the greatest dollar value, or save the most time do not necessarily provide the greatest economic return per joule. For example, among the tested tasks, engineering work delivers about 10% more net dollar value per task than software development. Yet software development delivers about 27% more net value per joule. Second, GLM 5.2 delivers the highest average net dollar value, at $72.27 per task using 0.430 MJ, equivalent in energy to about four hours of active laptop use. By contrast, Gemma 4 31B delivers 56% of that net dollar value using just 23% of the energy, achieving approximately 2.5 times the NVJ and 2.8 times the TSJ. Third, across four single-accelerator deployments, Qwen3.6-27B's NVJ varies by a factor of 4.32 and TSJ by 9.27, with B200 leading both. Additionally, one and eight H100s deliver similar net dollar value, but eight reduce completion time by 22% at 3.24 times the energy. Our framework makes output value and expert time saving under an energy budget explicit objectives for improving models, accelerators, and software, while offering a new basis for assessing AI's potential economic effect on occupational work.

</details>


### [179] [MOER: Mix-Policy Optimization under Expert Prefix Guidance with Selective Rollback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30873)
*Xuyang Zhong,Jiuding Yang,Yiwen Guo,Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MOER混合策略优化框架，通过衰减专家思维链前缀+选择性回滚+协调混合目标，将专家引导与自探索统一，稳定训练并取得最优平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合策略优化方法存在两类问题：静态全轨迹专家引导抑制自探索；专家前缀引导缺乏正确性保证，导致性能次优甚至训练崩溃。

Method: MOER向提示中注入衰减的专家思维链前缀，由模型补全剩余推理，前缀长度逐步退火以鼓励自探索；采用选择性回滚机制，将失败补全替换为原始完整专家轨迹以保证每条off-policy轨迹正确；设计协调的混合策略目标，对on-policy token使用GRPO损失，对专家前缀与补全token使用信赖域SFT损失。

Result: 在数学与通用领域推理任务上取得最高平均分，优于所有基础与混合策略基线，并在多种任务、模型规模和基座模型类别上保持一致泛化。

Conclusion: 专家引导与自探索可通过前缀退火、回滚正确性保障与协调目标有效融合，兼顾性能与训练稳定性，具备良好通用性。

Abstract: Mix-policy optimization incorporates off-policy expert data into the reinforcement learning (RL) loop to transcend the capability boundary of on-policy RL. However, existing methods either provide static full-trace guidance that suppresses self-exploration, or adopt expert prefix guidance without correctness guarantees, leading to suboptimal performance or even training collapse. In this paper, we propose MOER, a mix-policy optimization framework that harmonizes expert guidance with self-exploration. MOER injects a decaying expert chain-of-thought prefix into the prompt and lets the model complete the remaining reasoning, with the prefix length progressively annealed to encourage self-exploration. A selective rollback mechanism replaces failed completions with the original full expert trace, guaranteeing the correctness of every off-policy trajectory. Furthermore, a harmonized mix-policy objective optimizes on-policy tokens with a GRPO loss while supervising both expert prefix and completion tokens with a trust-region SFT loss, substantially stabilizing training. Extensive experiments show that MOER achieves the highest average scores in mathematical and general domain reasoning tasks, outperforming all basic and mix-policy baselines, and generalizes consistently across diverse tasks, model scales, and base model categories. Codes are available at https://github.com/CityU-MLO/MOER.

</details>


### [180] [One Teacher, Many Views: Reducing Reference Sensitivity in On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30880)
*Khouloud Saadi,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出现有on-policy self-distillation在完整参考解条件下存在"参考诱导的目标不稳定性"，并提出Monte Carlo Privileged Distillation（MCPD），将特权上下文建模为部分参考视图上的分布并蒸馏边缘化教师分布，在数学与科学推理基准上更稳定、更一致地优于全参考OPSD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OPSD方法通常只使用以完整参考解为条件的单一教师分布。但参考解只是推理过程的一种实现，它除提供答案证据外，还编码了特定的顺序、措辞与解题路径。因此全参考条件会生成依赖学生推理时不可得信息的目标。作者发现，在固定问题与学生状态的前提下，仅替换一个有效参考为另一个，也会显著改变教师分布（reference-induced target instability），这构成该形式化的核心缺陷。

Method: 提出MCPD：把特权上下文视为"部分参考视图"上的分布，而非单一完整参考；教师模型在多个视图下被评估，并对各视图下产生的下一token分布取平均，从而蒸馏边缘化（marginal）教师分布。作者论证该概率空间平均正是最小化特权视图上期望教师到学生KL散度所诱导的精确目标。

Result: 在数学与科学推理基准上，MCPD产生与参考更一致的监督信号，并一致性地优于全参考OPSD。

Conclusion: 用单一完整参考作为特权上下文会引入参考诱导的目标不稳定性；将特权上下文边缘化为部分参考视图的分布，并在概率空间平均教师分布，可得到理论上精确且经验上更稳健的自蒸馏监督，是改进OPSD的有效方向。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) improves reasoning by training a model on its own trajectories with dense token-level supervision from a privileged copy conditioned on a reference solution. Existing methods typically use a single teacher distribution conditioned on the complete reference. We identify a limitation of this formulation: a reference is only one realization of the reasoning process. Although it provides evidence about the answer, it also encodes an ordering, phrasing, and solution path. Full-reference conditioning can therefore produce correct targets that depend excessively on information unavailable to the student at inference. We show that replacing one valid reference with another, while holding the problem and student state fixed, can substantially alter the teacher distribution, a phenomenon we call reference-induced target instability. To address this instability, we introduce Monte Carlo Privileged Distillation (MCPD), which models privileged context as a distribution over partial reference views and distills the marginal teacher distribution. MCPD evaluates the teacher under multiple views and averages the resulting next-token distributions. We show that this probability-space average is the exact target induced by minimizing the expected teacher-to-student KL divergence over privileged views. Across mathematical and scientific reasoning benchmarks, we show that MCPD produces more reference-consistent supervision and consistently improves over full-reference OPSD.

</details>


### [181] [Inherited, Not Invented: Tracing the Provenance of Wait in Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30882)
*Zhizhuo Kou,Sizai Hou,Long Chen,Jian Yang,Junyu Luo,Jingshu Peng,Wenkai Zhang,Sirui Han,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过追踪"wait"标记在开放模型训练历程中的出现，指出该词的来源是训练数据（mid-training与推理轨迹微调）而非RL本身，零RL难以自发创造该标记；标记频率不能作为RL诱导推理的指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学界普遍认为RL教会了推理模型在重新审视步骤前写"wait"，但RL带来的大幅准确率提升并不依赖该词。作者希望区分"RL发现了什么"与"模型从训练数据中继承了什么"，避免混淆。

Method: 沿开放模型的训练历史追踪"wait"一词：使用无需标签的指标（频率、位置、形式、用法）及注入错误测试，均基于采样输出计算。

Result: 零RL几乎不会创造该标记：在缺乏该词的基座上，RL显著提升MATH-500准确率而"wait"仍近乎缺失。该词随训练数据出现——OLMo-3的mid-training及推理轨迹微调使其出现在多数原本缺失的模型中。基座已使用时，两种公开发布的RL-Zero配方（OLMo-3、MiMo）会放大其出现，而同基座的KL-free GRPO则不会；OLMo-3配方削弱其形式并使其成为更敏感但更宽松的告警；SFT后的RL几乎不改变它，而作者的GRPO配方会抹去由微调植入的标记。

Conclusion: 标记频率本身不能指示RL诱导的推理；应警惕以LLM作为评判者的修订指标，因为自动评判可能逆转人工标注的趋势。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is credited with teaching reasoning models to write {wait} before reconsidering a step, yet large RL accuracy gains do not require the word. This view can confuse what RL discovers with what a model inherits from its training data. We trace the word through open models' training histories, reading it with label-free indices of frequency, position, form and usage and an injected-error test, all computed from sampled outputs. We find that zero-RL almost never coins the marker: on bases that lack the word, RL raises MATH-500 accuracy substantially while {wait} stays near absent. It arrives with training data: mid-training in OLMo-3, and fine-tuning on reasoning traces installs it in most models whose base lacks it. Where the base already uses it, both released RL-Zero recipes (OLMo-3, MiMo) multiply it, whereas KL-free GRPO from the same base does not; OLMo-3's recipe wears down its form and makes it a more sensitive but more liberal alarm; RL after supervised fine-tuning barely changes it, and our GRPO recipe erases a marker seeded by fine-tuning. Finally, we show that marker frequency alone does not indicate RL-induced reasoning and caution against LLM-as-a-judge revision metrics, as automated judges can reverse human-labelled trends.

</details>


### [182] [Tracing Subliminal Learning Through Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30884)
*Angela Tang,Vishesh Anand,Gonçalo Paulo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究"潜意识学习"现象：学生模型从教师模型生成数据中会继承与数据无关的偏好痕迹，在硬标签与on-policy软标签蒸馏下均成立，且跨权重初始化、预训练数据顺序、语料、规模与架构持续存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有观察表明基于他模型生成数据训练的LLM会继承无关特征（subliminal learning），但尚不清楚该现象在硬标签蒸馏与on-policy软标签蒸馏两类设定下、跨模型差异（初始化、预训练语料与顺序、规模、架构）是否稳健，以及偏好信号的载体是什么、教师能力与学生习得能力是否相关。

Method: 使用Pythia、PolyPythia与DataDecide OLMo模型，分别考察(P1)硬标签蒸馏：学生用带偏好偏置教师生成的数字序列训练；(2)on-policy软标签蒸馏：学生自生成数据、教师logits作为监督信号。进一步构造与教师数字频率匹配但不依赖prompt与前序数字的合成序列以定位信号载体；在top-K软标签蒸馏中剔除动物词元并观察迁移变化；并跨预训练checkpoint比较教师传输与学生习得能力。

Result: 两种设定下迁移均稳健存在，且跨越权重初始化、预训练数据顺序、预训练语料、模型规模与架构差异；跨checkpoint上"传递偏好"与"习得偏好"能力分离发展（部分checkpoint更擅长当教师而非学生，反之亦然），角色互换时跨模型迁移幅度呈强不对称性；与prompt无关的合成数字序列在硬标签设定下仍引发显著迁移；top-K软标签中移除动物词元可降低（但同模型设定下未完全消除）迁移；软标签蒸馏在更少训练下产生更一致的迁移。

Conclusion: 潜意识偏好迁移是蒸馏中稳健且与具体数据分布无关的现象，仅凭输出频率统计即可部分承载信号；教师与学生的迁移能力相互独立，且不对称性提示信号传递机制的双向差异。由于on-policy蒸馏在后训练中日益普及，该结果为评估非预期偏好迁移提供了直接警示。

Abstract: Large language models trained on data generated by other models can sometimes inherit traits unrelated to that data, a phenomenon called subliminal learning. We study both hard label distillation, where students train on numbers generated by a biased teacher, and on-policy soft label distillation, where students generate the data and the teacher's logits provide the learning signal. Using Pythia, PolyPythia, and DataDecide OLMo models, we find that transfer persists in both settings across differences in weight initialization, pretraining data order, pretraining corpus, model size, and architecture. Across pretraining checkpoints, the ability to transmit a preference and the ability to acquire one develop separately: some checkpoints are more effective teachers but weaker students, and vice versa. Consistent with this distinction, cross-model transfer can be strongly asymmetric in magnitude when teacher and student roles are reversed. To investigate what carries the preference signal, we construct synthetic number sequences that match the teacher's generated number frequencies but have no dependence on the prompt or preceding numbers and find that these still induce substantial transfer in the hard-label setting. In top-$K$ soft-label distillation, removing animal tokens from the loss reduces transfer, although not completely in the same-model setting. In our experiments, soft label distillation produces more consistent transfer with less extensive training than hard label distillation. The growing use of on-policy distillation in model post-training makes this a particularly relevant setting for assessing unintended preference transfer.

</details>


### [183] [Building Presentation Intelligence for High-Quality Slide Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30888)
*Yuhang Xie,Cheng Li,Chenliang Li,Mingyu Xu,Mingwei Sun,Suhang Zheng,Ming Yan,Xiaoxiao Xu,Lin Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出面向"展示智能"的自动PPT生成统一训练框架，融合交叉验证数据合成、规则与先验知识引导的教师增强、混合教师SFT+轨迹过滤，以及可验证美学指标驱动的GRPO强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPT是主流沟通媒介，但高质量PPT制作高度依赖人工；LLM智能体虽带来新机遇，但自动PPT生成在数据质量、监督信号与视觉美观优化上仍存在挑战。

Method: 1) 构建可显式控制难度与有效性的交叉验证数据合成流水线；2) 基于规则与先验知识的教师增强框架提升教师模型指导能力；3) 混合教师策略与轻量级结果驱动轨迹过滤改进SFT；4) 提出一组可验证美学指标并结合GRPO强化学习优化幻灯片级视觉属性。

Result: 增强SFT使Qwen-3.6-27B与Qwen-3.6-35B-A3B在PresentBench上分别提升7.45与9.35分，超过GLM-5.2并保持良好泛化；RL在五个美学维度上进一步提升性能，弥补SFT在视觉精修上的不足。

Conclusion: 该统一框架有效支撑高质量PPT自动生成，SFT负责文本与内容能力、RL负责视觉美观优化，二者互补。

Abstract: PowerPoint presentations (PPTs) are a dominant medium for communication across diverse domains, yet high-quality PPT generation still depends heavily on labor-intensive human effort. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) for agentic tasks have created both new opportunities and challenges (Chen et al., 2026) for automatic PPT generation. In this work, we present a unified training framework for automatic PPT generation toward presentation intelligence, integrating cross-validated data synthesis, guided teacher enhancement, and verifiable reward optimization. We first construct a cross-validated pipeline for data synthesis, with explicit control over both difficulty and validity. We then develop a teacher-enhancement framework grounded in rules and prior knowledge to substantially strengthen the instructional capacity of teacher models. Building on this, we further improve supervised fine-tuning (SFT) through a mixed-teacher strategy and a lightweight outcome-driven trajectory filtering method. Finally, based on the resulting SFT models, we introduce a suite of verifiable aesthetic metrics together with a GRPO-based reinforcement learning framework to optimize slide-level visual attributes beyond text-only supervision. Experiments show that our enhanced SFT pipeline improves Qwen-3.6-27B and Qwen-3.6-35B-A3B by 7.45 and 9.35 points on PresentBench, respectively, surpassing GLM-5.2 while maintaining strong generalization. Reinforcement learning further improves performance across five aesthetic dimensions, compensating for the limitations of SFT in visual refinement. Overall, the results demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework for high-quality PPT generation.

</details>


### [184] [Same molecule, Different Knowledge: Cross-Identifier Binding Failures in LLMs For chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30903)
*Khouloud Saadi,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示LLM中同一分子的不同标识符（俗名、IUPAC名、SMILES）并不能等价访问知识，提出"跨标识符绑定失败"现象，并通过Binding Self-Distillation缓解，使模型可直接从SMILES检索事实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学AI工作流通常用多个共指标识符表示同一分子，并隐含假设这些表示可互换地访问LLM知识；作者要检验该假设是否成立。

Method: 构建受控基准测试（7B–72B基础模型及化学专用模型）；开展行为、表征与因果分析（内部表征、冲突标识符、参数编辑）；提出Binding Self-Distillation，利用训练中的词汇身份把模型已有知识访问迁移到结构路径。

Result: 假设不成立：共指标识符一致地暴露模型知识的不同子集；即使模型能从SMILES识别分子并经名称检索事实，该事实仍无法直接从SMILES获取；提升化学表示能力或编辑单个事实均不足以建立可靠的跨标识符访问。

Conclusion: 核心失败在于标识符与模型已有知识之间的绑定；Binding Self-Distillation无需名称查找或从零训练，即可在推理时直接从SMILES实现事实检索。

Abstract: Chemical AI workflows routinely represent the same molecule through multiple co-referring identifiers, including trivial names, IUPAC names, and SMILES, implicitly assuming that these representations provide interchangeable access to an LLM's knowledge. To test this assumption, we first introduce a controlled benchmark and show that it does not hold. Across foundation models spanning 7B to 72B parameters, even chemistry-specialized models, co-referring molecular identifiers consistently expose different subsets of the model's knowledge. We then conduct a series of controlled behavioral, representational, and causal analyses to characterize this failure. We show that even when a model can identify a molecule from its SMILES and retrieve a fact through the corresponding name, the same fact can remain inaccessible directly from SMILES. We term this phenomenon \emph{cross-identifier binding failure}. Further analysis of internal representations, conflicting identifiers, and parameter editing shows that neither improved chemical representation competence nor modification of individual facts is sufficient to establish reliable access across identifiers. Together, these results indicate that the core failure lies in the binding between an identifier and knowledge already present in the model. Motivated by this diagnosis, we introduce Binding Self-Distillation, a simple mitigation that uses lexical identity during training to transfer the model's existing knowledge access to the structural route. This enables factual retrieval directly from SMILES at inference time, without requiring a name lookup or training the underlying model from scratch.

</details>


### [185] [Random Initialization as a Baseline for Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30950)
*Pinar Sungu Isiacik,Georgiana Ifrim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过在UEA MTSC档案上对比四个TSFM家族共七个预训练实例与其架构匹配的随机初始化对照，冻结表征后用四种分类器评测183组配对比较，发现预训练带来的平均准确率提升仅约2.58个百分点，且经Holm校正后仅NuTime显著；ROCKET、Hydra、Quant等非预训练基线仍具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TSFM在下游MTSC任务上的评测往往将预训练权重与网络架构的贡献混为一谈，缺乏架构匹配的随机初始化对照与强非预训练基线，因而难以判断性能增益究竟来自自监督预训练表征还是架构本身。

Method: 选取四类TSFM家族中七个公开预训练实例，并为每个实例构造架构相同、随机初始化的对照模型；每个实例使用五种随机初始化，在UEA MTSC档案上提取冻结表征，分别用岭分类、逻辑回归、RBF SVM与随机森林四种分类器评估；剔除受计算约束影响的设置后共获得183组数据集级配对比较，采用Wilcoxon符号秩检验（含Holm校正）及整体合并检验，并与ROCKET、Hydra、Quant对比。

Result: 预训练对全部七个实例均提升平均准确率，但幅度有限，为1.43至5.19个百分点，总体平均提升2.58个百分点；七个实例中有三个的Wilcoxon原始p值低于0.05，但Holm校正后仅NuTime保持显著；183组配对整体合并检验p<0.001，但作者以校正后的逐实例检验为主要证据；ROCKET、Hydra、Quant在无需大规模预训练的情况下仍保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 预训练权重相对架构本身的增益较小且统计上不稳定，MTSC评测应区分预训练权重与底层架构的作用，并同时纳入架构匹配的随机初始化对照与强非预训练基线。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) use self-supervised pretraining to learn reusable representations for downstream tasks such as multivariate time series classification (MTSC). We examine how much pretrained weights contribute beyond the architecture itself by comparing seven publicly released instances from four TSFM families with architecture-matched randomly initialized counterparts on the UEA MTSC archive. For each instance, five random initializations are evaluated using four classifiers on frozen representations: ridge classification, logistic regression, RBF SVM, and random forest. After model-specific exclusions due to computational constraints, the study comprises 183 paired dataset-level comparisons. Pretraining improves mean accuracy for all seven instances, but the gains are modest, ranging from 1.43 to 5.19 percentage points, with an overall average improvement of 2.58 points. Three of seven per-instance Wilcoxon signed-rank tests have raw $p$-values below 0.05, but only NuTime remains significant after Holm correction. A secondary pooled test across all 183 pairs gives $p < 0.001$, while the corrected per-instance tests remain the primary evidence. We also compare with ROCKET, Hydra, and Quant, which remain competitive without requiring large-scale pretraining. These findings suggest that MTSC evaluations should distinguish the effect of pretrained weights from that of the underlying architecture and should include both architecture-matched random initialization and strong non-pretrained baselines.

</details>


### [186] [OpenAttention: One Percent Is Enough with Tail Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30951)
*Hyunwoo Yu,Jongbeom Lee,Hyunbin Kim,Sungjun Park,Jaemin Choi,Jincheol Yang,Seunghun Moon,Bongjoon Hyun,Kyomin Sohn,Kyeongbo Kong,Suk-Ju Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 OpenAttention，用少量"尾部代理"近似稠密注意力分布，配合免训练 Query-Geometry 聚类与选择器，在 1% 读取率下保持近全稠密精度并显著加速解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏注意力只读取少量 KV cache，未读的尾部如何处理是关键难题：直接丢弃会引入偏差，而以往按采样处理尾部在 5% 及以下读取率时失效——此时尾部可能藏有选择器漏掉的重要 token（heavy hitters）。

Method: 核心观察：用一个将整个尾部折叠为计数感知均值的单一代理，其输出误差在各读取率下均低于采样。进一步用闭式分析证明低读取率下采样会使权重误差近乎翻倍于丢弃，而单一代理保持在其下。据此提出 OpenAttention 流程：聚类 → ErrorGate → top-k 选择 → 计数感知均值代理。为高效实现聚类与选择，设计免训练的 Query-Geometry Clustering（QGC）与 Query-Geometry Selector（QGS），在压缩 keys 时尊重 query 方向；ErrorGate 不以质心排序簇，而是按每个簇的估计权重误差分配精确读取预算。

Result: 在 Llama-3.1-8B 上、RULER-32K HARD 基准中，1% 读取率下保留 99% 稠密精度；在多种模型、上下文长度与数据集上达到 SOTA。定制注意力 kernel OpenAttention.flash 在 1% 读取时解码注意力阶段比稠密注意力快 10×。

Conclusion: 以计数感知均值代理替代采样处理稀疏注意力的尾部，可在极低读取率下保持精度，并通过几何感知的免训练聚类/选择与误差驱动的预算分配实现高效实现与显著加速。

Abstract: Sparse attention reads only a small subset of the key-value cache, and how to treat the unread tail remains a key challenge. Discarding the tail biases the output, and sampling it as previous approaches do fails at reads of 5% and below, where the tail likely holds heavy hitters the selector missed. Our key observation is that the simplest possible proxy, one token that folds the entire tail into its count-aware mean, already yields lower output error than a sample at every read behind a practical selector. We further show in closed form that at low reads sampling nearly doubles the weight error of discarding while a single proxy stays below it. Building on these findings, we explore approximating the dense attention distribution with a few proxies that target the tail. We propose OpenAttention, a pipeline of clustering, ErrorGate and top-k selection followed by a count-aware mean proxy. To implement clustering and selection both efficiently and effectively, we design training-free Query-Geometry Clustering (QGC) and a Query-Geometry Selector (QGS), which compress the keys while respecting the query directions. Additionally, unlike prior methods that rank clusters by centroid alone, ErrorGate allocates the exact read by the estimated weight error of each cluster. On RULER-32K HARD with Llama-3.1-8B, OpenAttention retains 99% of dense accuracy at a 1% read. It also achieves state-of-the-art accuracy across models, context lengths and datasets. Our customized attention kernel, OpenAttention.flash, runs the decode attention stage 10$\times$ faster than dense attention at a 1% read.

</details>


### [187] [Ground-Truth-First Benchmarking of Reinforcement Learning via Converse Optimality: From Smooth, Fully Observed Systems to Non-Smooth or Partially Observed Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30954)
*Grégoire Ouerdane,Sinan Ibrahim,Stefan Streif,Pavel Osinenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"真值优先"(ground-truth-first)的强化学习基准框架,基于逆最优性(converse optimality)构造带有认证最优值函数与策略的任务,仅用于评估而不向学习器公开,从而将性能度量从相对回报排序转变为可观测的最优性差距、遗憾与策略误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL基准通常是相对的:在最优值未知的环境中按回报排名,距离最优性不可观测,结论易受奖励设计、随机性、超参数、环境选择与模拟器细节影响。

Method: 基于逆最优性构建统一框架,含两种模式:(1)敏感性基准——从连续时间生成器采样认证环境以评估对任务变化的鲁棒性;(2)oracle参考比较——用原生离散时间生成器按最优性差距、遗憾、策略误差评估策略。扩展包括:虚拟输入回拉处理非仿射原始动作接口与有限协方差非高斯扰动;非光滑逆最优性支持带折点值函数与切换面的认证基准;部分可观测下用信息状态将基准归约为充分统计量上的逆最优性,并提供信息惩罚参考。

Result: 在串行平面机械臂、动态扩展非完整车辆与四旋翼类任务上复现了涵盖优化器敏感性与oracle参考比较的定量结果。

Conclusion: 显式区分连续时间(物理可解释但依赖步长Δt与数值格式)与原生离散时间(环境、oracle、学习器共享同一转移律,消除外部积分混淆)两类建模范式,提供了可认证、可复现的RL评估方法,代码与诊断工具公开。

Abstract: Benchmarking in reinforcement learning is usually relative: algorithms are ranked by returns on environments with unknown optima. Distance to optimality remains unobservable, while conclusions may depend on reward design, stochasticity, hyperparameters, environment selection, and simulator details. We develop a ground-truth-first framework based on converse optimality, constructing each task with a certified optimal value function and policy withheld from learners and used only for evaluation. The framework unifies two modes: sensitivity benchmarking samples certified environments from continuous-time generators to assess robustness to task variation, while oracle-referenced comparison uses native discrete-time generators to evaluate policies via optimality gap, regret, and policy error. Continuous-time models support physically interpretable sensitivity studies but introduce dependence on the simulator step ($Δt$) and numerical scheme. Native discrete-time models align the environment, oracle, and learner on the same transition law, eliminating external integration choices as a confounder. We reproduce quantitative results covering optimizer sensitivity and oracle-referenced comparisons on serial planar arms, dynamically extended nonholonomic vehicles, and quadrotor-inspired tasks. The methodology extends beyond smooth affine Gaussian systems: virtual-input pullback handles non-affine raw-action interfaces and finite-covariance non-Gaussian disturbances. A non-smooth converse optimality formulation enables certified benchmarks with kinked value functions and switching surfaces. For partially observed evaluation, an information-state formulation reduces benchmarking to converse optimality on a sufficient statistic and provides an information-penalty reference. Code, fixtures, and diagnostics are available on the public project page.

</details>


### [188] [Doubly Robust Distributionally Robust Offline Contextual Pricing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30957)
*Min Xu,Xinyi Yin,Yunfan Zhang,Yuxuan Han,Houcai Shen,Caihua Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对连续定价场景下离线分布鲁棒评估与学习，提出双重鲁棒（DR）框架：评估端用局部化DR估计量（仅需少量回归）实现半参数有效；学习端利用需求噪声光滑性处理收益结果的定价特异不连续，得到 s=1,2 时 Õ_p(T^{-s/(2s+1)}) 的遗憾界，优于现有连续处理DRO-OPL，实验验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线上下文定价依赖观测日志数据，训练与部署环境存在分布漂移；DRO是OPE/L的合理工具，但现有方法多限于离散动作。已有的连续处理IPW型DRO估计量对倾向得分收敛速率敏感（尤其非参数估计时），导致估计误差与遗憾偏大。

Method: 构建连续定价下分布鲁棒OPE/L的双重鲁棒框架。评估：提出局部化DR估计量，通过只拟合少量回归（与标准非鲁棒DR相当）来规避最坏情况期望的计算难题，并在温和乘积速率条件下达到半参数有效性。学习：利用需求噪声的固有光滑性应对收益结果中与定价相关的不连续（如阈值型购买决策），并给出有限样本遗憾界。

Result: 在光滑阶数 s=1,2 下获得 Õ_p(T^{-s/(2s+1)}) 的有限样本遗憾界，改进了现有连续处理DRO-based OPL的遗憾速率；评估估计量在半参数意义下有效。多层级分布漂移下的广泛实验验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提DR框架在连续定价的分布鲁棒OPE/L中兼顾计算可行性与统计效率，并带来更优的遗憾速率，为分布漂移下的离线定价提供了理论与实证支持。

Abstract: Offline contextual pricing often relies on logged observational data, but faces challenges from distributional shifts between training and deployment environments. Distributionally robust optimization (DRO) provides a principled approach to off-policy evaluation and learning (OPE/L). However, existing methods are mostly limited to discrete actions. Recent work has explored DRO for continuous treatments using inverse propensity weighting (IPW), while such IPW-based estimators can be sensitive to the convergence rate of propensity score estimates, particularly when estimated nonparametrically, which may lead to larger estimation errors and regret. In this work, we develop a doubly robust (DR) framework for distributionally robust OPE/L in continuous pricing settings. For evaluation, we propose a localized DR estimator that addresses the computational challenges of worst-case expectations by fitting only a small number of regressions, comparable to standard non-robust DR, while achieving semiparametric efficiency under mild product rate conditions. For learning, we leverage the inherent smoothness of demand noise to handle pricing-specific discontinuities in revenue outcomes (e.g., threshold-based purchase decisions), establishing a finite-sample regret bound of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}_p(T^{-s/(2s+1)})$ for smoothness orders $s=1,2$. This bound improves upon existing regret rates in existing DRO-based off-policy learning (OPL) for continuous treatments. Extensive experiments under various levels of distribution shift validate our proposed framework.

</details>


### [189] [Energy and Latency First: How Deployment Constraints Reverse Time-Series Model Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30965)
*Shruti Bothe,Illyyne Saffar,Ayush K. Varshney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对蜂窝网络切换预测这一真实部署场景，指出仅按精度排序的时间序列模型忽略了硬性时延与累积能耗约束，并提出在尾部时延可行性筛选后按实测推理能耗排序的评估协议。实验显示梯度提升树（F1=0.974）既最准又最省能，而仅按精度排第一的CNN-1D（F1=0.914）在真实占空比部署下能耗高出3倍以上；作者给出该结论可外推的三个条件，说明精度导向的基准可能把不可部署的模型排到首位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI/时间序列模型的排名通常只看精度，而实际部署受制于排名中未体现的约束。论文选取约束明确且可度量的蜂窝网络切换预测场景：推理须在每个40ms测量周期内于终端侧完成且早于网络动作，超时预测会被其所预测的测量值取代，同时能耗是贯穿设备生命周期的累积预算。这两项约束均不在模型选择目标中出现，因此需要研究部署约束下的合理评估与排序方式。

Method: 构建一套评估协议：先在时延分布的尾部（而非均值）筛选截止期可行性，再在可行模型中按实测推理能耗排序，且能耗计量包含前向传播之前的特征计算（通常被省略）。在该协议下评测八种架构，并对比"仅按精度排名"与"部署约束下排名"的差异；采用包含占空比循环的真实部署模式测量能耗。

Result: 基于手工时序特征的梯度提升树精度最高（F1=0.974），且运行成本处于最低之列；而纯精度基准会排第一的CNN-1D精度较低（F1=0.914），在真实占空比部署模式下每次推理能耗高出3倍以上。作者进一步给出该结果应当成立的三个条件，使其结论可推广到电信之外的领域。

Conclusion: 仅按精度的基准不仅低估部署成本，还可能把无法部署的模型排到第一名；在存在硬时延与累积能耗约束的实际场景中，应以尾部可行性筛选加实测（含特征计算）能耗排序来选择模型，且该发现的适用范围超出通信领域。

Abstract: AI models, and time-series models in particular are typically ranked by accuracy and deployed under constraints absent from that ranking. In this paper, we study a real setting where these constraints are explicit and measurable: handover pre- diction in cellular networks, where inference runs on the User Equipment once per 40 ms measurement period and must complete before the network acts. The timing bound is hard: a late prediction is superseded by the measurement it was predicting, while energy is a cumulative budget spent for the life of the device. Neither appears in the objective these models are selected on. We evaluate eight architectures under a protocol that screens for deadline feasibility at the tail rather than the mean, then ranks the feasible models on measured inference energy, count- ing the feature computation that precedes the forward pass and is usually omitted. Gradient-boosted trees on handcrafted temporal features are the most accurate model (F1=0.974) and among the cheapest to run, while the neural architecture an accuracy-only benchmark would rank first (CNN-1D, F1=0.914) costs over 3 times more energy per inference under a realistic duty-cycled deployment pattern. We give three conditions under which this outcome should be expected, so the finding applies beyond telecommunications. Accuracy-only benchmarking does not merely under-report deployment cost. It can rank first a model that cannot be deployed.

</details>


### [190] [EEG-Captioner: Grounding EEG-to-Language Generation in Fundamental Signal Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30995)
*Leyuan Jin,Jiahao Mei,Yixuan Yuan,Shimeng Li,Mengyue Wu,Yue Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出带信号级QA标注的EEG-语言数据集（160,781段）与双域EEG编码器及EEG-Captioner统一框架，信号级QA达到88.81% Macro-BA，caption事实一致性由82.06%提升至83.46%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG-to-language模型生成的语言看似合理，却无法证明模型真正学会解读EEG信号；作者认为需要通过可客观测量的信号属性来探针式评估模型的理解能力。

Method: 构建160,781段EEG-语言数据集，每段配信号级QA（覆盖五类属性族，涉及频谱、空间、时间、侧向属性并需定量比较）及特征接地的EEG caption；提出联合建模时域波形与频谱-空间表示的双域EEG编码器；并推出EEG-Captioner统一框架，同时以信号级QA与caption监督训练。

Result: 双域编码器在对比的EEG编码器中取得最高整体宏平均平衡准确率；EEG-Captioner在信号级QA上Macro-BA达88.81%，caption事实一致性从82.06%提升至83.46%。

Conclusion: 信号级QA监督与caption监督具有互补收益，能同时提升EEG信号理解的可验证性与生成文本的事实一致性。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG)-to-language models offer a promising approach for translating complex neural signals into human-readable descriptions. However, generating plausible language does not necessarily demonstrate that a model has learned to interpret the underlying EEG signal. A direct way to assess such interpretation is to probe objectively measurable properties of the signal itself. We therefore construct an EEG-language dataset of 160,781 segments annotated with signal-level question answering (QA) pairs, where each question targets one of five property families and requires a quantitative comparison involving spectral, spatial, temporal, or lateral signal properties. Each segment is also paired with a feature-grounded EEG caption. We further propose a Dual-Domain EEG Encoder that jointly models temporal waveforms and spectral-spatial representations, and introduce EEG-Captioner, a unified EEG-to-language framework trained with both signal-level QA and EEG caption supervision. Experiments show that the proposed encoder achieves the highest overall macro-averaged balanced accuracy (Macro-BA) among the compared EEG encoders, while EEG-Captioner achieves a Macro-BA of 88.81% on signal-level QA and improves caption factual consistency from 82.06% to 83.46% over caption-only language adaptation, demonstrating the complementary benefits of signal-level QA and caption supervision.

</details>


### [191] [XALIGN: A Human-centric Visual Analytics Approach to Understanding AI Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31015)
*Bahavathy Kathirgamanathan,Gennady Andrienko,Natalia Andrienko,Tatiana von Landesberger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出XALIGN——一种并排展示规则解释与多种特征归因方法（TreeSHAP、KernelSHAP、LIME）的可视分析工作流与界面，将方法间的一致与矛盾作为可探索信号，用于高风险领域专家对照领域知识校验模型预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法多以忠实性、鲁棒性等技术指标评估，但在高风险应用中专家须将预测与既有知识核对；不同解释方法常相互不一致，而单独查看每种方法时这种分歧难以察觉。

Method: 设计XALIGN视觉分析工作流与界面，对同一随机森林模型并排呈现规则型解释与TreeSHAP、KernelSHAP、LIME等归因结果，显式可视化方法间的一致与矛盾，并支持用户探索、与领域知识比对。在德国信用数据集与气象数据集（基于ICON模式输出与Cloudnet观测事件分类）两个案例中应用，并进行三位专家的专家评估。

Result: 两个案例中，视图均揭示了单一方法无法显现的规则解释与归因方法之间的差异；专家评估中，参与者认为XALIGN有助于识别重要特征并避免对预测的过度信任，但无XAI背景的参与者认为将规则与归因关联较为困难。

Conclusion: 多方法并排对比可将解释分歧转化为可利用的信号，提升专家对模型的审慎信任；但界面仍需降低跨方法（规则—归因）关联的认知门槛，以适配非XAI背景用户。

Abstract: Explainable AI (XAI) methods are usually evaluated for technical properties such as faithfulness and robustness. In high-stakes application domains, experts also need to check model predictions against established knowledge. Different explanation methods often disagree, and this disagreement is hard to see when each method is inspected separately. We propose XALIGN, a visual analytics workflow and interface that shows rule-based explanations and several feature attribution methods (TreeSHAP, KernelSHAP, and LIME) side by side for the same Random Forest model. Agreements and contradictions between the methods become visible signals that users can explore and compare with domain knowledge. We apply XALIGN in two case studies: the German Credit dataset and a meteorological dataset, in which events derived from ICON model output and Cloudnet observations are classified. In both, the views reveal discrepancies between the rule-based explanation and the attribution methods that a single method would not show. In an expert evaluation with three specialists, participants found XALIGN useful for identifying influential features and for avoiding over-trust in predictions. Linking rules to attributions was harder for the participant without an XAI background.

</details>


### [192] [Federated Differentially Private Optimization via Low-Rank Preconditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31069)
*Antti Koskela,Tejas Kulkarni,Elias Raninen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出联邦差分隐私PCA(DP-PCA)框架，在安全聚合下实现线性通信成本的集中式DP保证，用于GLM的Hessian近似与预条件DP梯度下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦场景下DP优化的协方差/Hessian估计存在矛盾：现有中心DP方法通信代价随维度平方增长，而线性通信的基线仅为本地DP且随客户端数增加而退化。

Method: 在安全聚合下构建联邦DP-PCA：包含DP秩选择、DP幂迭代(power method)，以及对投影到所选低维子空间的协方差做加噪释放；以此协方差估计作为广义线性模型的Hessian近似，并用于低通信预条件DP梯度下降。

Result: 给出协方差估计的误差界以及相应低通信预条件DP梯度下降的误差界；实验上在远低于二次通信成本的开销下匹配中心化基线。

Conclusion: DP-PCA可在保持集中式DP保证的同时将通信成本降至线性，是联邦DP优化中兼顾隐私、通信与精度的一种可行方案。

Abstract: We consider differentially private (DP) optimization in the federated setting, where a DP estimate of the feature covariance matrix serves as a Hessian approximation for generalized linear models. Existing central DP methods for covariance estimation have communication cost that grows quadratically in the data dimension, while existing baselines with linear communication cost are only locally private and degrade as the number of clients increases. As a remedy, we propose a federated DP-PCA framework that achieves linear communication cost while providing central DP guarantees under secure aggregation. It consists of a DP rank selection step, a DP power method, and a noisy release of the covariance projected onto the selected low-dimensional subspace. We give error bounds for this covariance estimate and for the resulting low-communication preconditioned DP gradient descent. Empirically, our proposed DP optimization method matches a centralized baseline with quadratic communication cost at a fraction of the communication.

</details>


### [193] [When Forgotten Facts Return: Relearning-Aware Unlearning of Knowledge Graph Embeddings via Tangent Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31075)
*Parnian Fazel,Mohammad M Maheri,Hamed Haddadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文首次研究知识图谱嵌入中的"再学习"现象：在持续训练下，近似遗忘的事实会重新浮现，R基于实体嵌入共享；提出 Kernel-Anticipated Unlearning (KAU)，利用切核建模持续训练对遗忘事实打分的一阶响应并嵌入遗忘损失，使编辑方向更难被后续训练逆转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近似遗忘通过编辑实体嵌入而非重训来响应删除请求，但嵌入会随新事实持续更新。在这种常规持续训练下，被遗忘事实的恢复程度高于精确遗忘基线（未见过这些事实重训后接受同样持续训练）。作者将此再学习归因于遗忘事实与新事实共享的实体嵌入，并指出针对深度网络的抗再学习方法未考虑该恢复背后的图结构。

Method: 提出 Kernel-Anticipated Unlearning (KAU)：用切核（tangent kernel）建模持续训练如何经由共享参数改变遗忘事实的分数；将该一阶响应写成闭式表达，并在遗忘损失内部对其求导；从而把嵌入编辑导向持续训练较少逆转的方向。与最先进的知识图谱遗忘方法及抗再学习方法在相同持续训练下对比，以超出精确遗忘基线的泄漏量为指标，覆盖三个骨干模型。

Result: 在三个骨干模型上，KAU 相对最强知识图谱遗忘方法，将泄漏在 FB15k-237 上降低 46–63%，在 CoDEx-L 上降低 50–82%，同时保持更高的效用（retained utility）。

Conclusion: KAU 通过在遗忘目标中显式建模并最小化持续训练的一阶反作用，有效缓解知识图谱嵌入的再学习/泄漏问题，且在效用保持上优于基线，表明将图结构下的参数耦合纳入遗忘设计是必要且有效的。

Abstract: Approximate unlearning answers a deletion request on a knowledge-graph embedding by editing entity embeddings instead of retraining from scratch. These embeddings keep being updated as new facts arrive. Under this ordinary continued training, forgotten facts recover more than in the exact-unlearning baseline, a model retrained without them and given the same continued training. We trace this relearning to entity embeddings shared by forgotten and new facts. To our knowledge, this is the first study of relearning in knowledge-graph embeddings. Relearning-robust methods developed for deep networks do not account for the graph structure underlying this recovery. We propose Kernel-Anticipated Unlearning (KAU), which uses the tangent kernel to model how continued training changes forgotten facts' scores through these shared parameters. KAU expresses this first-order response in closed form and differentiates through it inside the forgetting loss. The objective steers edits toward directions that continued training reverses less. We compare KAU with state-of-the-art knowledge-graph unlearners and relearning-robust methods under identical continued training, measuring leakage beyond the exact-unlearning baseline. Across three backbones, KAU reduces the strongest knowledge-graph unlearner's leakage by 46-63% on FB15k-237 and 50-82% on CoDEx-L, at higher retained utility.

</details>


### [194] [nnInteractive Agent: General-Purpose AI Agents as 3D Image Annotators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31080)
*Fabian Isensee,Constantin Ulrich Harsy,Maximilian Rokuss,Benjamin Hamm,Philipp Vollmuth,Klaus H. Maier-Hein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出开源 nninteractive-agent：把交互式 3D 分割模型 nnInteractive 通过 MCP 封装为工具，并附上"专家提示技能"，使通用 AI 智能体（Claude Code、Codex）无需额外训练即可端到端完成"理解请求—定位目标—提示分割—检查与纠错—响应反馈"的全流程；14 幅多模态图像、51 个目标的基准上，仅凭自然语言请求平均 Dice 达 0.81/0.84，给出目标位置后达 0.85。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式 3D 分割模型（如 nnInteractive）虽能把少量空间提示（点、涂鸦、框）转为精确体素掩膜，但"找结构、放提示、查结果、纠错"的人工标注工作仍由人承担，属于瓶颈。与此同时，通用 AI 智能体已具备看图、调工具、写代码的能力，因此作者希望让智能体接管这部分人工标注劳动，且不引入额外训练。

Method: 以 Model Context Protocol（MCP）将 nnInteractive 暴露为工具集，并提供一份编码资深用户提示经验的 skill；用户以自然语言提出请求，智能体自主解析分割意图、定位目标、构造空间提示、调用 nnInteractive、检视 3D 结果、自我纠错并对用户反馈迭代；复杂需求（如计数肝病灶）由智能体自行编写代码搜寻候选、并在被要求时拆分粘连病灶。在覆盖 CT、MRI、PET 与 micro-CT（人、小鼠、蚂蚁；14 幅图像、51 个目标，含器官、肿瘤、肌肉、足骨、血管树、昆虫脑）的基准上，与文本提示式 3D 模型 VoxTell 及"使用参考分割放置提示的 oracle"对比。

Result: Claude Code 与 Codex 仅凭请求即达平均 Dice 0.81 与 0.84；被告知目标位置后两者均达 0.85，提示效果接近 oracle。VoxTell 在多数标准放射学目标上更准，但鲁棒性较差：对其中一类目标输出空掩膜，并在腿部肌肉、足骨、小鼠与蚂蚁上失败，而智能体在这些场景仍保持准确。

Conclusion: 通用智能体可在零额外训练下自主驱动交互式 3D 分割模型完成复杂体数据标注，兼具精度与跨模态/跨物种鲁棒性，是迈向自动化 3D 医学影像标注的可行路径；其价值在于工具封装与提示技能的工程化组合，而非新模型训练。

Abstract: Interactive 3D segmentation models such as nnInteractive turn a few spatial prompts (points, scribbles, boxes) into accurate volumetric masks, but a human still does the annotation work: finding each structure, placing prompts, checking the result and correcting it. General-purpose AI agents can now interpret images, call tools and write code. We show that such agents can take over this work without additional training: the user makes a request in plain language, and the agent works out what has to be segmented, locates the targets, prompts nnInteractive, inspects the 3D result, corrects it and responds to feedback. Asked for 'the muscles used for chewing', an agent identifies and segments the masseters and pterygoids; asked to count liver lesions, it writes its own code to find candidates and splits a merged lesion on request. Our open-source nninteractive-agent provides nnInteractive as tools over the Model Context Protocol (MCP), together with a skill that encodes how experienced users prompt it. On a benchmark spanning CT, MRI, PET and micro-CT of humans, a mouse and an ant (14 images; 51 targets from organs and tumours to muscles, foot bones, vessel trees and an insect brain), Claude Code and Codex reach a mean Dice of 0.81 and 0.84 from the request alone; when told where each target is, both reach 0.85 and prompt nearly as effectively as an oracle that places prompts using the reference segmentation. A text-prompted 3D model (VoxTell) is more accurate on most standard radiological targets but less robust: it returns an empty mask for one of them and fails on the leg muscles, the foot bones, the mouse and the ant, where the agents remain accurate. Code: https://github.com/MIC-DKFZ/nninteractive-agent

</details>


### [195] [Transformers with CoT Discover Heavy-Ball Acceleration Through Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31089)
*Yuxuan Cai,Yuan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在上下文权重预测任务中，Transformer经梯度下降教师初步监督后，再用真实回归权重做轨迹级监督，会自发实现Heavy-Ball算法，超越教师算法并加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（Huang et al., 2025）将Transformer视为算法执行器，证明其CoT可模仿梯度下降教师；本文追问CoT能否超越单纯模仿教师算法，即训练能否让模型发现新算法。

Method: 沿用线性回归中的上下文权重预测任务。先用梯度下降教师监督CoT步骤，随后加入基于真实回归权重的轨迹级监督，并理论分析训练后模型所实现的更新规则。

Result: 轨迹级监督后模型实现Heavy-Ball（重球法），相对原梯度下降教师获得加速，且该加速算法在无任何Heavy-Ball教师监督下涌现。

Conclusion: 提供了一个具体理论实例：Transformer推理可通过训练发现新算法而非仅模仿被监督算法，为CoT理论理解提供新视角。

Abstract: Modern transformers often solve complex tasks by generating and refining intermediate reasoning steps. To develop a theoretical understanding of chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning, a notable recent study by Huang et al. (2025) viewed transformers as algorithm executors, and showed that when transformers' CoT steps are supervised by a gradient-descent teacher, they can learn to implement gradient descent for in-context weight prediction in linear regression. In this paper, we study the same in-context weight prediction task but ask whether CoT reasoning can go beyond merely imitating the teacher algorithm. We show that, after initial supervision from a gradient-descent teacher, further trajectory-level supervision using the true regression weights leads the model to implement the Heavy-Ball method, thereby achieving acceleration over the original teacher. Notably, this accelerated algorithm emerges without supervision from any Heavy-Ball teacher. Our results provide a concrete theoretical example in which transformer reasoning can discover new algorithms through training, rather than merely imitate the algorithm used for supervision.

</details>


### [196] [What Comes Next and Why: Interpretable Next-Event Prediction with Neuro-Symbolic Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31114)
*Tim Nico Bauerschmidt,Isabel Valera,Jilles Vreeken*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出神经符号模型RULES从数据中直接学习时序规则，并将其与残差网络组成为RUNES，在保持竞争性精度的同时提升下一个事件预测的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列事件预测方法偏重精度而牺牲可解释性；神经符号方法虽能通过学习符号规则提升可解释性，但受时序依赖影响，难以扩展到下一事件预测任务。

Method: RULES：神经符号模型，直接从数据学习序列规则；RUNES：将学习到的规则与残差网络加性组合，规则部分提供透明的活跃规则，残差网络对预测进行调整。

Result: RULES在合成数据上能准确恢复真实规则，在真实数据上发现有紧凑、可解释的规则集；RUNES在提供活跃规则及残差调整透明信息的同时达到竞争性精度。

Conclusion: 规则学习与残差网络加性结合是兼顾序列事件预测精度与可解释性的有效途径。

Abstract: In real-world applications, being able to explain why a prediction is made is often as important - if not more so - than achieving high accuracy. However, existing methods for sequential event prediction typically prioritize accuracy at the expense of interpretability. Neuro-symbolic approaches offer a promising direction to improve interpretability by learning symbolic rules, but extending them to next-event prediction is challenging due to the presence of temporal dependencies. We introduce RULES, a neuro-symbolic model that learns sequential rules directly from data, and combine it with a residual network to form RUNES (Rule Network for Event Sequences). RULES accurately recovers ground truth rules from synthetic data and discovers compact, interpretable rule sets on real data. RUNES combines these rules additively with a residual network, achieving competitive accuracy while providing transparent access to active rules and how residuals adjust predictions.

</details>


### [197] [Is High Local Accuracy Enough? Geometric Consensus with Support-Adaptive Statistical Personalization for Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31133)
*Junyu Lin,Bosong Qi,Pengcheng Shi,Jiayuan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对个性化联邦学习（PFL）中本地精度掩盖全局与稀有类泛化缺陷的问题，提出兼顾泛化与个性化的评估协议与统一框架FedGRC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PFL实际部署需适应异构客户端，同时保持超出本地分布的识别性能；但高本地精度会掩盖对本地稀有或缺失类别的失败，聚合指标也会遮蔽表现最差客户端的弱点，因此需要更严格的评估与能同时提升全局泛化与个性化下限的方法。

Method: 提出补充本地精度的评估协议（全局、稀有类、未见类评估，并以最差客户端衡量个性化下限）；提出FedGRC框架，含三部分：(i) Consensus-Guided Representation Learning (CGRL)，通过共享的方向与幅度引导学习判别性表征；(ii) Multilayer Metric Synthesis (MMS)，基于多层表征构建共享协方差度量用于统计分类；(iii) Support-Adaptive Shrinkage Personalization (SASP)，按本地样本支撑度将本地类均值向共享均值收缩，在保留共享知识的同时实现自适应。

Result: 在3个数据集、5种异构设置下与21种方法对比并做消融实验：CIFAR-10与CIFAR-100上，全局识别与个性化下限分别至少超过SOTA基线2.17%和2.13%；真实异构FedRS数据集上，本地适应、全局识别与个性化下限分别超过各自最佳基线2.15%、3.30%与4.61%。

Conclusion: FedGRC通过共享表征与度量的统计分类结合支撑度自适应收缩，在提升全局识别的同时抬高个性化下限，验证了兼顾泛化与个性化的PFL设计有效性。

Abstract: Real-world deployment of personalized federated learning (PFL) requires adapting to heterogeneous clients while preserving recognition performance beyond local distributions. However, high local accuracy can conceal failures on locally rare or absent classes. Effective personalization should therefore strengthen global recognition while raising the personalization floor. We propose an evaluation protocol that complements local accuracy with global, rare-class, and unseen-class evaluations to reveal generalization obscured by local scores, and assesses personalization floors among the worst-performing clients to expose weaknesses hidden by aggregate performance. To meet these deployment challenges, we propose FedGRC, a unified PFL framework integrating generalization and personalization through (i) Consensus-Guided Representation Learning (CGRL), which learns discriminative representations through shared directional and magnitude guidance; (ii) Multilayer Metric Synthesis (MMS), which constructs a shared covariance metric from these multilayer representations for statistical classification; and (iii) Support-Adaptive Shrinkage Personalization (SASP), which adapts class means within this metric by shrinking local estimates toward shared means according to local sample support, enabling adaptation while retaining shared knowledge. Extensive experiments, including benchmarking against 21 methods across 5 heterogeneity settings on 3 datasets and comprehensive ablation studies, validate the effectiveness of FedGRC. On CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, FedGRC surpasses state-of-the-art (SOTA) baselines in global recognition and personalization floors by at least 2.17% and 2.13%, respectively. On the real-world heterogeneous FedRS dataset, it outperforms the respective best baselines by 2.15%, 3.30%, and 4.61% in local adaptation, global recognition, and the personalization floor.

</details>


### [198] [PriMa-Causa: Causal Prescriptive Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31147)
*Felix Saretzky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PriMa-Causa，将因果推理嵌入规范性维护（prescriptive maintenance），使维护动作按估计的干预效应排序；它由因果推理层、参数化产线仿真器、图条件表格因果基础模型三部分组成，可在单次前向传播中回答干预查询，处方误差低于关联性基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制造数据为观测性数据，关联性预测模型估计的是关联量而非干预量，可能误导维护动作选择且预测误差无法揭示该偏差；而传统因果估计器需逐案专家主导的因果研究，受限于有限的决策时间窗。

Method: 构建PriMa-Causa：(1) 将干预效应估计整合进PriMa参考模型的因果推理层；(2) 提供制造业数据中缺失的干预真值的参数化仿真器；(3) 在该合成产线先验上一次性预训练的图条件表格因果基础模型，单次前向传播完成干预查询。

Result: 处方误差低于关联性基线，在弱重叠与稀疏上下文条件下优势最大；部分披露的因果图可进一步降低误差。

Conclusion: 合成先验带来的因果归纳偏置与图条件化可替代逐案估计器拟合，是面向自主与自愈维护的因果推断自动化的一步。

Abstract: Automated manufacturing requires data-driven decisions under uncertainty and sparse observational records, and unplanned downtime is a dominant cost of getting them wrong. Prescriptive maintenance recommends a maintenance action against an impending failure rather than merely predicting it. Established reference architectures couple predictive models with rule- or optimisation-based recommendation logic. Selecting a corrective action, however, requires an interventional quantity: how the line behaviour would change if the action were set. Manufacturing data is observational, so a model fitted to predict the outcome estimates an associational quantity and may misdirect the choice of action without the prediction error revealing it. Causal estimators target the interventional quantity but require an expert-led causal study per case, for which the bounded decision window of operation leaves no room. This dissertation analyses how causal reasoning can be embedded into prescriptive maintenance so that actions are ranked by their estimated interventional effect. The result is PriMa-Causa, built from three components: a causal reasoning layer that integrates the estimate into the PriMa reference model, a parametric simulator that supplies the interventional ground truth absent from manufacturing data, and a graph-conditioned tabular causal foundation model that, pre-trained once on this synthetic production-line prior, answers interventional queries in a single forward pass. PriMa-Causa attains a lower prescription error than associational baselines, with the largest margin under weak overlap and sparse context, and a partially disclosed causal graph reduces this error further. The causal inductive bias of the synthetic prior and the graph conditioning can thus replace per-case estimator fitting, a step toward automating causal inference for autonomous and self-healing maintenance.

</details>


### [199] [Towards Field-Deployable Wearable IMU Systems for Occupational Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31160)
*Ihsan Ul Haq,James Brusey,Farnad Nasirzadeh,Chandan Karmakar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 基于VTT-ConIoT基准，用统一LOSO协议首次系统分析该数据集对配置的敏感性：6种分类器×5种传感器位置×3种窗长，并附个性化校准试点与表格GAN/时序增强对比。LOSO个体精度61.9%–88.7%（均值77.5%，差27pp）；多传感器融合与长窗有利，标准增强小幅稳定增益，CTGAN反而有害；弱受试者仅用自身30%数据校准可提升16.8pp。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴IMU的施工人员活动识别文献常报告>95%的聚合精度，且少有真正的跨受试者（LOSO）评估，掩盖了个体间方差，无法反映实际部署表现；同时缺乏对VTT-ConIoT数据集的系统配置敏感性分析与增强方法可靠比较。

Method: 采用单一LOSO（留一受试者）协议，在VTT-ConIoT上组合6种分类器、5种传感器佩戴位置、3种滑动窗长进行系统性配置敏感性分析；辅以个性化校准试点实验；并对基于表格GAN（CTGAN）与标准时序增强方法做经有效性验证的对比。

Result: 主配置（多传感器、16类）逐受试者LOSO精度为61.9%–88.7%，均值77.5%，个体间极差达27个百分点（数据均为实验室采集）；多传感器融合与更长窗长均提升精度；标准时序增强带来小幅但一致的增益；CTGAN增强显著降低精度，并给出分布保真度机制解释；个性化校准使最弱受试者仅用自身30%数据即恢复16.8pp。

Conclusion: 聚合精度掩盖显著的个体差异，应以LOSO与逐受试者指标作为报告基准；建议采用多传感器融合与较长窗长、优先标准时序增强而非表格GAN合成；个性化校准是缩小弱受试者差距的高性价比手段，为职业体域传感网络向现场部署扩展提供有证据支撑的配置指导。局限在于数据为实验室采集，野外泛化仍需验证。

Abstract: Wearable inertial measurement unit (IMU) systems for construction worker human activity recognition (HAR) routinely report aggregate accuracies above 95 %, rarely under genuine subject-independent (leave-one-subject-out (LOSO)) evaluation, masking the per-individual variance that determines real-world deployment performance. Using the VTT-ConIoT benchmark, we conduct, to our knowledge, the first systematic configuration sensitivity analysis of this dataset: six classifiers across five sensor placements and three window sizes under a single LOSO protocol, a personalised-calibration pilot, and a validated comparison of tabular GAN-based and standard time-series augmentation. Per-subject LOSO accuracy for the main multi-sensor, 16-class configuration ranges from 61.9 % to 88.7 % (mean 77.5 %), a 27 pp spread, though all data are laboratory-collected. Multi-sensor fusion and longer windows both improve accuracy; standard augmentation gives small, consistent gains, while Conditional Tabular GAN (CTGAN) augmentation actively harms accuracy with a demonstrated distributional-fidelity mechanism; and personalised calibration recovers 16.8 pp for the weakest participants from just 30 % of their own data. These findings provide evidence-grounded configuration guidance for scaling occupational body sensor networks towards field deployment

</details>


### [200] [Inside-JEPA: From Prediction to Relational Grounding in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31194)
*Hossein Resani,Javen Qinfeng Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Inside-JEPA，在JEPA潜状态中为有序实体对设置关系通道，并证明逆向动力学损失（需头部读取这些通道）能在无关系标签下将通道接地为真实关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA在潜空间学习世界模型，规划高效但状态不透明；作者希望让潜状态中的关系信息可解释、可接地。

Method: 在JEPA潜状态内为有序实体对引入关系通道；用逆向动力学损失（从通道变化恢复动作）监督，头部单独读取通道或读取完整状态；在Something-Something V2、RH20T机器人任务上评测，并用现成检测/跟踪做五类关系迁移；辅以打乱标签、两种无标签目标、5%动作标签等消融。

Result: 仅潜预测与随机关系头持平；逆向动力学监督使关系AUROC提升（十个带框设定+.065~.103，RH20T留出任务+.081，五关系检测跟踪+.050~.061）；但实体潜变量上的逆头解码动作更准却几乎不改善接地；打乱标签、无标签目标、5%标签均失败；给定动作时预测关系变化接近观测未来（.599 vs .621）；一步关系目标下16通道监督读出在43%情形将正确动作排入65个中的前五（仅预测潜在24%、频率先验17%）。

Conclusion: 关系通道的可接地性依赖逆向动力学监督与头部对通道的读取，而非单纯潜预测精度；该机制为JEPA提供了可解释的关系表征与目标导向动作选择能力。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) learn world models in latent space, enabling efficient planning but leaving their states opaque. We introduce Inside-JEPA, which places relational channels for ordered entity pairs inside the JEPA latent state and shows that an inverse-dynamics loss can ground these channels without relation labels when its head reads them, either alone or as part of the full state. On Something-Something V2, latent prediction alone performs no better than a random relation head, while inverse-dynamics supervision that recovers actions from channel changes improves relational AUROC by .065-.103 across ten settings with annotated boxes, by .081 on RH20T robot episodes with held-out tasks, and by .050-.061 across five relations using off-the-shelf detection and tracking. An inverse head on entity latents decodes actions more accurately but barely improves relational grounding; shuffled labels, two label-free objectives, and action labels on only 5% of the pairs also fail to ground the channels. Given an action, predicted relational changes approach the quality of observed future relations (AUROC .599 vs .621). For one-step relational goals, a supervised readout of the 16 channels ranks the correct action among the top five of 65 in 43% of cases, compared with 24% for prediction-only latents and 17% for a frequency prior.

</details>


### [201] [Evolving Federated Density Peak Clustering with Shuffler-Based Anonymization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31211)
*Miha Ožbot,Seiichi Ozawa,Igor Škrjanc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于演化模糊聚类与shuffler匿名化的单轮联邦聚类方法eFDPC，仅传输密度峰值以降低通信与隐私泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦聚类每轮通信都会向服务器暴露额外模型信息，增加推断本地数据属性的风险，即便不共享原始样本；因此需要减少客户端-服务器交互轮次并实现不可链接性，使传输信息无法归属到单个客户端。

Method: 将演化模糊数据流聚类与密度峰值聚类结合于联邦框架：各客户端在私有小批量数据上通过簇的添加与合并独立演化微簇；对学到的微簇施加密度峰值聚类，仅上传选中的峰值而非完整本地模型；客户端与服务器之间引入基于shuffler的匿名层，在重构与全局峰值选择前对贡献进行匿名化。给出仅峰值变体eFDPC-P以及带服务器端标签传播的eFDPC-G。

Result: 在合成与真实基准数据集上，eFDPC在单轮（one-shot）设置下优于近期联邦聚类基线，并可扩展到更高维、非IID数据。

Conclusion: 单轮通信结合峰值选择性传输与shuffler匿名化，可在保证不可链接性的同时实现有效的联邦聚类，且对高维非IID场景具良好可扩展性。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a novel one-shot federated clustering approach based on evolving fuzzy clustering and shuffler-based anonymization. In federated clustering, each round reveals additional model information to the server and can increase the opportunity to infer properties of local datasets, even when raw samples are never shared. Consequently, fewer client-server exchanges and unlinkability are important, so transmitted information cannot be attributed to any single client. Data-stream clustering algorithms, which learn in a single pass over the data, align well with the one-shot federated learning paradigm, where a single communication round can be sufficient. The proposed Evolving Federated Density Peak Clustering (eFDPC) method combines evolving fuzzy data-stream clustering and density peak clustering in a federated setting. Each client independently evolves micro-clusters from private data mini-batches through cluster addition and merging. To limit communication, density peak clustering is applied to the learned micro-clusters and only the selected peaks are transmitted, rather than the full local models. A shuffler-based layer is introduced between the clients and the server to anonymize contributions prior to reconstruction and global peak selection. We consider a peak-only variant, eFDPC-P, as well as eFDPC-G with server-side label propagation. Experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmark datasets show that eFDPC outperforms recent federated clustering baselines in one-shot settings and scales to higher-dimensional, non-IID data.

</details>


### [202] [Local Discrimination of Binary Classifiers via Localized AUC Functional](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31216)
*Wenye Ren,Anna Győrffy-Kerekes,Tancredi Dorsi,Olga Demler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Localized AUC（LAUC）指标及基于其的LOCATOR方法，用于识别风险预测模型预测不准确的患者亚群，并在真实数据与模拟中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUC等整体指标在评估时掩盖了性能异质性，使临床决策者无法察觉模型在某些患者亚群上的失效，可能导致不良临床决策。

Method: 采用加权AUC的一种变体——Localized AUC（LAUC），并据此构建LOCATOR方法，以定位模型预测不准确的患者队列；同时为LAUC在此用途下的使用建立统计理论基础。

Result: 在真实世界数据与模拟实验中均测试了该方法，验证其能够识别出模型预测不准确的患者亚群。

Conclusion: LAUC与LOCATOR可将评估从单一整体性能扩展到亚群层面的异质性诊断，为识别风险预测模型的局部失效提供可行工具。

Abstract: Risk prediction models are frequently evaluated with AUC, which means that during evaluation we are blind to performance heterogeneity, which could lead to bad decision-making in clinics. In this work, we aim to change that by identifying patient cohorts for whom the risk prediction model might not be an accurate predictor. To separate these cohorts, we use a version of the weighted AUC, which we call Localized AUC or LAUC. Using this metric, we build a method that we coin LOCATOR. This method is able to identify cohorts of patients for whom the risk prediction model is inaccurate. We test this method both in real-world data and in simulations. We also build a statistical background for the use of Localized AUC for our purposes.

</details>


### [203] [QuaNAS: Uncertainty-Aware Reuse of Earlier Searches for Query-Efficient Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31251)
*Brama Seta Putra Satria,Dwi Sunaryono,Misbakhul Munir Irfan Subakti,Irzal Ahmad Sabilla,Laras Suciningtyas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QuaNAS将同一搜索空间内的历史搜索库视作可靠性不确定的先验知识，结合主效应与零成本代理构建贝叶斯先验，并用高斯过程在线校准先验可信度，从而在新数据集仅允许数十次短训练的条件下显著降低anytime regret。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新数据集上的NAS通常只允许数十次短训练，因此"首次评估之前搜索已掌握的知识"决定了搜索效率。但多数NAS方法从零开始，至多依赖跨任务不可靠的零成本代理，缺乏可量化、可复用的先验。

Method: 将同一搜索空间的既有搜索库作为显式知识来源：以设计决策的主效应为主要先验、零成本代理为次要先验，无需新数据集任何标签即可给出候选架构的先验分布；高斯过程从先验最优点出发，在每次评估后重新估计各先验的可信程度（在线权重再校准）。评估采用在看确认数据之前冻结的协议，一次性在12个新数据集（图像、时序、文本、表格）上测试。

Result: 短训练目标上的anytime regret相对五个预指定的从零开始基线降低2.7–3.9倍（Holm校正后 p=0.0012）；最终模型在9个数据集上与同配方参考模型持平（非劣界0.5个百分点）或更优，且未做任何评估即选出的先验最优点亦如此。事后比较显示：无搜索库时同一高斯过程的regret升高一倍以上；主效应池化适用于弱迁移的表格任务，逐任务代理适用于图像、时序、文本任务及小规模或不匹配的搜索库。主效应还使迁移知识可解释、可检查。

Conclusion: 在有限短训练预算下，显式利用历史搜索库并对其可靠性做在线贝叶斯校准，可大幅提升NAS效率并保持模型质量；迁移机制的选择应视任务可迁移性与库规模而定，且主效应形式赋予迁移知识可检查性。

Abstract: Neural architecture search (NAS) on a new dataset often affords only tens of short training runs, so what the search knows before its first evaluation decides its efficiency. Yet most NAS methods start from scratch, guided at best by zero-cost proxies that are unreliable across tasks. QuaNAS treats a library of earlier searches in the same search space as explicit knowledge of uncertain reliability. Main effects of design decisions learned from this library and, secondarily, zero-cost proxies become priors over candidate architectures without any label of the new dataset. A Gaussian process starts at the prior optimum and re-estimates after every evaluation how far each prior is trusted. Under a protocol frozen before any confirmation data were seen, QuaNAS was evaluated once on 12 new image, time-series, text and tabular datasets. It lowered anytime regret on the short-training objective 2.7- to 3.9-fold relative to five pre-specified NAS baselines that start from scratch (Holm-adjusted p = 0.0012 each). Its final models matched (non-inferiority margin 0.5 pp) or exceeded matched-recipe reference models on nine datasets, as did the prior optimum, selected without a single evaluation. Post-hoc comparisons with established transfer methods given the same library trace the gain to the library: without it, the same Gaussian process had over twice the regret. They also show where each transfer mechanism works best: the pooled main effects of QuaNAS for weakly transferable tabular tasks, per-task surrogates for image, time-series and text tasks and for small or mismatched libraries. Finally, the main effects make the transferred knowledge inspectable.

</details>


### [204] [DISC: Domain-Integrated Similarity Cost for Time-Series Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31288)
*Yen-Ku Liu,Hongjie Chen,Franck Dernoncourt,Ryan Rossi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出可学习的相似度聚合框架 DISC，用校准迁移观测来选取、加权并组合多种时间序列数据集聚相似度指标，使相似度分数与下游预测误差的对齐程度优于单一指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列数据集与基础模型快速增长，使数据集比较成为流程核心环节；在模型适配前评估源-目标迁移性需要能概括迁移相关关系的相似度估计，而无需对每个候选对做穷尽评估。现有多种相似度指标捕获的数据集关系不同、与下游效用的对齐程度不一，存在明显缺口。

Method: 在分布外与迁移学习设定下，测度源-目标相似度分数与下游预测误差之间的相关性以量化上述缺口；进而提出 Domain-Integrated Similarity Cost（DISC），一个可学习聚合框架，利用校准迁移观测为不同下游任务选择、加权并组合多个相似度指标。校准后仅凭相似度指标值即可对源-目标对评分。

Result: 在所报告的划分上，DISC 通过整合互补指标值更好地区分对下游行为重要的源-目标数据集差异，其与下游预测误差的对齐程度高于单指标基线。

Conclusion: 时间序列数据集相似度具有任务依赖性；学习式指标整合比人工挑选单一相似度指标能更好地代理下游迁移行为。

Abstract: The growth of time-series datasets and foundation models has made dataset comparison a central part of time-series workflows. Assessing source-target transfer before model adaptation requires similarity estimates that can summarize transfer-related dataset relationships without exhaustive source-target evaluation over every candidate pair. Many metrics have been proposed for measuring similarity between time-series datasets, but they capture different dataset relationships and vary in how well they align with downstream utility across tasks. We evaluate this gap by measuring the correlation between source-target similarity scores and downstream forecasting errors in out-of-distribution and transfer-learning settings. We propose Domain-Integrated Similarity Cost (DISC), a learnable aggregation framework that uses calibration transfer observations to select, weight, and combine similarity metrics for different downstream tasks. By integrating complementary metric values, DISC better distinguishes source-target dataset differences that matter for downstream behavior. After calibration, DISC scores source-target pairs using only their similarity-metric values and yields higher alignment than single-metric references on the reported split. These results show that time-series dataset similarity is task-dependent, and that learned metric integration provides a stronger proxy for downstream transfer behavior than manually selecting one similarity metric.

</details>


### [205] [The Bayesian Learning Rule Beyond KL Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31308)
*Sophia Sklaviadis,Wu Lin,Andre Martins,Mario Figueiredo,Thomas Moellenhoff,Emtiyaz Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出BLR并非只能在KL几何下成立：对混合空间Bregman/Fenchel-Young discrepancy类可导出加性信息几何优化算法，得到广义BLR及其规范的梯度更新，并给出Tsallis负熵下的q-高斯后验实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典BLR利用贝叶斯更新与KLD的关系，为指数族变分后验导出高效加性更新，但KL几何具有局限性：当后验族具有参数依赖支撑集时目标可能未定义。已有基于f-divergence的工作认为加性BLR计算仅KLD可行，本文要质疑并放宽这一限制。

Method: 将分析推广到混合空间Bregman或Fenchel-Young discrepancy类，证明其可导出加性信息几何优化算法；这类discrepancy诱导广义最大熵族、对偶坐标与广义BLR的canonical gradient更新，并在极限情形下退回经典BLR。

Result: 证实存在不能由KL型BLR导出的新算法；以Tsallis负熵结合二次统计量为例，广义BLR产生有界支撑与重尾的q-高斯后验，并具备类Newton的简便实现；CIFAR-100实验显示支撑集与尾部行为可控且性能有竞争力。

Conclusion: 加性BLR计算并不专属于KL几何，广义BLR拓展了可用几何与后验族，兼具理论统一性与实用价值。

Abstract: The Bayesian learning rule (BLR) exploits the relation between Bayesian updating and the Kullback-Leibler divergence (KLD) to derive efficient additive updates for exponential family variational posteriors. The KL geometry is powerful but restrictive: combined with posterior families with parameter-dependent support it can lead to undefined objectives. We ask which non-KL geometries preserve the additive BLR computation. Previous works that use $f$-divergences suggest that this is only possible with the KLD. Here, we show that this is not true. We show that additive information-geometric optimization algorithms can be derived for the class of mixed-space Bregman or Fenchel-Young discrepancies. These discrepancies induce generalized maximum-entropy families, dual coordinates, and generalized-BLR canonical gradient updates, recovering the classical BLR. The generalized BLR leads to new algorithms that \textit{cannot} be derived from the KL-based BLR. For the Tsallis negentropy with quadratic statistics, the generalized BLR yields bounded-support and heavy-tailed $q$-Gaussian posteriors and an easy Newton-like implementation. Experiments up to CIFAR-100 illustrate controllable support and tail behavior and competitive performance.

</details>


### [206] [Extreme Compression of Per-Layer Embeddings via Cross-Layer Latent Vector Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31309)
*Jiacheng Liu,Yaxin Luo,Xiaohan Zhao,Peng Sun,Xinyi Shang,Jiacheng Cui,Yonina C. Eldar,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OPTIC，将Per-Layer Embeddings压缩约100×–1000×，在约100×时仍保留97.8%性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型正同时扩大嵌入容量与Transformer计算量，Per-Layer Embeddings（PLE）这类做法使参数量占比很高（文中Gemma 4 E4B中占35.2%），因此高压缩率价值上升。在极端压缩率下，传统量化器只能为每个token-层向量分配仅几字节的独立码，效果受限。

Method: 提出OPTIC（Output-Path-fitted, Token-shared Indexed Codes）：每个token只存储一组量化码元组，在每一层用层专属仿射解码器解码；码与解码器均拟合PLE的输出路径而非被存储的向量本身，实现token共享、跨层复用。

Result: BF16 E4B在约100× PLE压缩下，七项基准平均保留97.8%性能；最强传统基线乘积量化仅66.3%。594×与1002×压缩下分别保留90.5%与84.1%。W4A16官方发布版在约100×压缩下保留96.9%。推理时约100×压缩下GPU显存减少29.7%，速度最多慢0.40%。

Conclusion: 面向输出路径的token共享索引码可在极端压缩率下显著优于传统量化，并以极小速度代价换取明显显存节省，适用于嵌入参数占比持续增长的语言模型。

Abstract: Scaling embedding capacity alongside Transformer computation is an emerging trend in language models. This trend makes high compression rates increasingly useful. Hence, we study extreme compression of Per-Layer Embeddings (PLE), which follow this trend and account for 35.2% of the parameters of Gemma 4 E4B. At such rates, conventional quantizers give each token-layer vector its own code of only a few bytes. We instead develop OPTIC (Output-Path-fitted, Token-shared Indexed Codes), which stores one quantized code tuple per token and decodes it at every layer with a layer-specific affine decoder. Codes and decoders are fitted to the PLE output path rather than to the stored vectors.
