### [240] [Detection Is Not Enough: Reasoning Traces as Testable Program Hypotheses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30200)
*Vaidehi Hegde*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 在冻结的LLaMA-2-7B-chat上检验无参考解条件下单条推理轨迹的可验证程度：检测并非瓶颈，修复才是开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型部署时不存在参考答案，推理轨迹是判断答案的唯一证据；现有无参考替代方案各有缺陷（自评损害原答案、采样一致性只衡量稳定性、扰动实验依赖人工模板），因此研究在无参考解、无评判模型、无权重更新条件下单条轨迹可验证的上限。

Method: 在GSM8K上对冻结的LLaMA-2-7B-chat做轨迹自检与AUROC评估；提出候选答案衍生的变形探针（metamorphic probe）：将轨迹抽象为基于题目数量的程序，扰动其中一个量、执行程序，并对改写后的问题仅查询模型一次，使每条轨迹自带测试；再分析检测器精度阈值D/(R+D)与重试策略的收益。

Result: 轨迹自检AUROC仅达0.741：三分之二的错误轨迹未陈述任何错误等式（计划错误但执行正确），且对错误计划类失败，检测器标记错误轨迹的频率低于正确轨迹。1,319道测试题中937道（71.0%）存在有效扰动；其中847道有可解析首条轨迹的，被支持的轨迹正确率为60.6%，基线为31.6%，但探针也否定了45.5%的正确轨迹。所有测试策略均未带来统计显著精度提升；修复率过低，同等重试预算下即使完美检测器也仅能挽回约66/1,319题。

Conclusion: 检测不是无参考轨迹验证的瓶颈，结构性限制在于错误多源于计划而非等式；重试策略需满足检测精度超过D/(R+D)才划算，而当前修复率使检测收益受限，修复（而非检测）是待解决的核心问题。

Abstract: When a language model is deployed, no reference solution exists, so the reasoning trace it writes is the only evidence for judging its answer. The reference-free substitutes each fail differently: self-assessment degrades the answer it was meant to protect, sampling agreement measures stability rather than correctness, and perturbation studies read the expected answer off a human-written template, grading a model rather than a trace. We ask how far a single trace can be verified with no reference solution, no judge model and no weight update, and find, for a frozen LLaMA-2-7B-chat on GSM8K, that detection is not what limits such a pipeline. Checking a trace against itself reaches an AUROC of 0.741 and stops there for a structural reason: two thirds of wrong traces state no false equation, having executed a mistaken plan correctly, and on wrong-plan failures the detector flags a wrong trace less often than a right one. A candidate-derived metamorphic probe exposes many of these failures: we abstract the trace into a program over the question's quantities, perturb one quantity, execute the program, and ask the model the rewritten question once, so every trace supplies its own test. Valid perturbations exist for 937 of 1,319 test questions (71.0%); among the 847 that also have a parseable first trace, supported traces are correct 60.6% of the time against a 31.6% base rate, although the probe also refutes 45.5% of correct traces. No tested policy yields a statistically significant improvement in accuracy. A flag earns its retry only when detector precision exceeds D/(R+D), where R and D are the rates at which a retry repairs a wrong trace and damages a correct one; our repair rate is so low that, at the same retry budget, even a perfect detector would recover an estimated 66 of 1,319 questions. Repair, not detection, is the open problem.

</details>


### [241] [A Consolidated Deployment Edition of a Microservice Core-Banking Platform: Composing Services by Reference into Three Hosts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30212)
*Tomisin Fagbola*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出并评估一个多租户 .NET 核心银行平台的"合并部署版"：将五个 API 服务通过项目引用组合进单一 API 主机，与消费者主机、后台主机共同构成三主机架构。通过 AWS 部署仿真对比，合并版在暴露全部 844 个操作、保持业务结果与权限行为一致的前提下，读取吞吐提升 2.3 倍、内存占用减半、服务任务计算成本降低 11–24%，但扩大了故障域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务带来独立部署与扩容能力，但每个已部署服务都会预留计算资源并增加运维负担。论文关注在中小规模多租户银行平台中，是否可以在不放弃原始仓库与服务代码的前提下降低这类"微服务税"，因此考察以"项目引用组合"替代独立部署的第二种部署版本。

Method: 以同一代码提交构建两套部署版本：原版为 9 个 AWS ECS Fargate 任务；合并版将 5 个 API 服务以项目引用方式组装进一个 API 主机，另有消费者主机与后台主机，服务代码仍留在各自原始仓库。论文描述了组合机制、模块清单、启动检查、请求处理与配置优先级，并在模拟 AWS 拓扑、任务规格与配置的仿真环境中进行对比：功能上核对 844 个已声明操作、47 个金融工作负载用例、五类主体的 231 个读探针、11 个跨租户与写授权用例；性能与成本上比较读吞吐、内存与服务任务计算成本；可靠性上注入 16 个故障，统计启动检查与行为测试的检测能力。

Result: 合并版暴露全部 844 个已声明操作；47 个金融工作负载用例中两版观测结果与账本完全一致；231 个读端点结果中 230 个匹配，唯一差异归因于仿真负载均衡器；11 个跨租户与写授权用例在两版中均符合预期。仿真中合并版读吞吐为原版的 2.3 倍，内存占用不足一半，按目录价格估算的服务任务计算成本低 11–24%（不含共享网关、负载均衡器、数据存储及其他运营成本）。可靠性方面，单一 API 主机崩溃将使五个模块同时离线，故障域扩大；16 个注入故障中，启动检查检出 10 个，行为测试额外检出 5 个，1 个逃逸。

Conclusion: 项目引用式组合可在保持服务代码与仓库结构不变的情况下，以显著更低的计算资源与成本提供等价的功能与授权行为，代价是故障域集中。结论限定于单一平台及镜像其拓扑、任务规格与配置的仿真部署，并非生产 AWS 实测，外推需谨慎。

Abstract: Microservices give teams independent deployment and scaling, but each deployed service also reserves compute and adds operational work. This paper examines a second deployment edition of a multi-tenant .NET core-banking platform. The existing edition runs as nine AWS ECS Fargate tasks; the alternative composes the five API services by project reference into one API host, alongside a consumer host and a background host. The service code remains in its original repositories, and both editions continue to be built from the same commits. We describe the composition mechanism, module manifest, start-up checks, request handling and configuration precedence, then compare the editions in an emulation of the AWS deployment. The composed edition exposes all 844 declared operations. Across 47 financial workload cases, both editions produced the same observed outcomes and matching ledgers. In read probes across five principal types, 230 of 231 endpoint results matched; the remaining difference was associated with the emulated load balancer. Eleven cross-tenant and write-authorisation cases also produced the expected outcomes in both editions. In the emulation, the composed edition delivered 2.3 times the read throughput and used less than half the memory. Estimated service-task compute at list prices was 11-24% lower, excluding the shared gateway, load balancer, data stores and other operating costs. The consolidation also enlarges the failure domain: one API-host crash takes all five modules offline. Of 16 injected faults, the edition's checks detected 10, behavioural tests detected five additional faults, and one fault escaped both. These findings concern one platform and an emulated deployment that mirrors its topology, task sizes and configuration; they are not production-AWS measurements.

</details>


### [242] [JANUS: Agentic Cross-Language Fuzzing Harness Generation for Android Native Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30218)
*Xiangyu Zhang,Yucheng Su,Lingling Fan,Yinan Li,Sen Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: JANUS：首个面向Android原生库的跨语言JNI感知模糊测试harness合成智能体方法，通过迭代解析跨语言调用依赖与自底向上递归构造参数对象，在44款应用上生成6094个有效harness（约为SOTA的5.9倍），并在五家厂商预装应用中发现6个未知漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Android应用广泛通过JNI在Java/Kotlin与C/C++间互操作，使内存安全的Runtime暴露于不安全的原生代码，形成被反复利用的攻击面。模糊测试虽有效，但现有harness生成工具难以忠实复现闭源跨语言API用法：(i) 跨JNI边界的精确程序分析难以扩展，轻量近似又会遗漏关键调用模式；(ii) 无人处理JNI方法所需复杂复合对象的构造。

Method: 提出JANUS，首个面向Android原生库的跨语言JNI感知模糊测试harness合成的智能体（agentic）方法。它迭代解析显式与隐式跨语言调用依赖以恢复应用特定运行时上下文，并以自底向上方式递归组装跨语言参数对象；借助自主智能体工作流减少对手工启发式的依赖，挖掘闭源原生库中隐含的API使用知识。

Result: 在44款流行真实应用（含Google、YouTube Music，共21,386个JNI方法）上，JANUS生成的有效harness数量（6,094）超过SOTA自动harness工具的5.9倍；在五家主流Android厂商（小米、Vivo、三星等）预装应用中发现6个此前未知的闭源原生库漏洞。

Conclusion: JANUS表明智能体化的工作流可有效克服跨JNI边界分析的扩展性与近似精度矛盾，并能系统化构造复合参数对象，从而显著提升闭源Android原生库模糊测试的harness生成效率与漏洞发现能力。

Abstract: Modern Android apps extensively rely on native libraries, bridging Java/Kotlin and C/C++ through the Java Native Interface (JNI). This cross-language interoperation, however, exposes the memory-safe Android Runtime to the unsafe C/C++ world, creating an attack surface that has been repeatedly exploited for full device compromise. While fuzzing is a proven technique for vetting native libraries, state-of-the-art harness generation tools struggle to faithfully capture closed-source cross-language API usage: (i) precise program analysis across the JNI boundary does not scale, whereas lightweight approximations miss critical invocation patterns; and (ii) none tackles the construction of complex composite objects consumed by JNI methods.
  In this paper, we propose JANUS, the first agentic approach to synthesizing cross-language JNI-aware fuzzing harnesses for Android native libraries. JANUS iteratively resolves both explicit and implicit cross-language call dependencies to recover app-specific runtime context, and recursively assembles cross-language argument objects in a bottom-up manner. JANUS leverages an autonomous agentic workflow to reduce reliance on hand-crafted heuristics, capturing the vast landscape of hidden API usage knowledge embedded in closed-source native libraries. Evaluation on 44 popular real-world apps (such as Google and YouTube Music, covering 21,386 JNI methods total) shows that JANUS produces over 5.9 times as many valid harnesses (6,094) as state-of-the-art automatic harnessing tools. On pre-installed apps from five mainstream Android vendors (e.g., Xiaomi, Vivo, Samsung), JANUS further uncovers 6 previously unknown vulnerabilities in closed-source native libraries.

</details>


### [243] [Agentic Architecture of Information Systems. The AAM/5C Model: A Reference Model for Distributed Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30221)
*Laurent Goujon*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该书提出AAM/5C概念参考模型，用单一授权规则R-AUT在七层架构中分离提议、决策与执行，并给出可反驳假设、形式化片段与验证计划，但无实证结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型组织在数百个交互组件的规模下面临"认知复杂度墙"：维护依赖图谱、以事件节奏响应，已超出人工团队能力。

Method: 概念性建模：五项能力（Capture、Comprehend、Choose、Coordinate、Control）实例化于L1–L7七个逻辑层；授权规则R-AUT分离提议方（L4概率规划器）、决策方（L6治理层，签署策略并生成签名评估证明）、执行方（L5安全层签发与证明绑定的短期act token，由策略执行点校验后由目标层落实）。给出显式保证契约、Rego授权策略、peer共识的部分TLA+规范，并在测量前固定反驳协议。

Result: H7a、H8a两条分析性命题由构造成立；十项假设（H1–H10）中其余命题（含日志完备性H7b、执行点不可绕过H8b）可被反驳。提出三个待验证新构件：分布式自治循环中的Validation步骤、临时权威面、认知依赖平面。书中未报告实证结果。

Conclusion: 贡献在于规范性参考模型：在单一授权规则下整合已知机制，并与自主计算、多智能体系统、企业架构及MCP/A2A等智能体协议进行定位比较，配套量化里程碑的验证计划；所有主张仍待验证。

Abstract: Large organizations increasingly face what this book calls the cognitive complexity wall: at the scale of hundreds of interacting components, maintaining the dependency map and reacting at the pace of incidents exceeds what a human team can deliver. This conceptual volume proposes AAM/5C, in which five capabilities (Capture, Comprehend, Choose, Coordinate, Control) are instantiated across seven logical layers, L1 to L7. A single authorization rule, R-AUT, separates who proposes, who decides and who executes: a probabilistic planner (L4) proposes; a governance layer (L6) decides under signed policies and records its verdict in a signed evaluation proof; a security layer (L5) issues a short-lived act token bound to that proof; a policy enforcement point verifies both before the target layer materializes the act. Under an explicit guarantee contract, two analytical statements (H7a, H8a) hold by construction. The other statements of its ten hypotheses (H1 to H10), including log completeness (H7b) and non-bypass of the enforcement point (H8b), are refutable, and the protocol that could refute them is fixed before any measurement. The contribution of the book is a prescriptive reference model that articulates, under this single authorization rule, known mechanisms and adds three constructs, all to be validated: the Validation step of the Distributed Autonomy Loop, the ephemeral authority surface and the cognitive dependency plane. The book positions them against autonomic computing, multi-agent systems, enterprise architecture and agent protocols (MCP, A2A), gives an authorization policy in Rego and a partial TLA+ specification of peer consensus, and sets out a validation program with quantified milestones. It reports no empirical results. This English edition is a translation of the French original, produced with the assistance of AI tools and validated by the author.

</details>


### [244] [The Customer Is the Builder: How AI Coding Agents Are Rewriting the Economics of Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30307)
*Ripon Chandra Malo,Zimeng Yu,Tong Qiu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该文提出"软件替代前沿"概念，用公开数据比较AI编程智能体"自建能力"的增速与市场变化指标，发现能力快速提升（基准任务时长约每129天翻倍）、开源替代广泛存在、部分企业已因自建而放弃采购，但整体采购份额未明显转移，提示购买行为的转变可能先于市场层面的可见信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编程智能体可能改变组织"买软件"还是"自建功能"的决策，但两者孰快孰慢缺乏系统证据；作者关注拥有领域知识、能自行描述与评估工具的组织型专业买家，试图刻画客户自建对商业软件采购的潜在替代压力及其可观测程度。

Method: 界定"软件替代前沿"（在可接受标准下自建维护成本低于购买的功能集合）；利用公开数据对比"自建能力"增长与市场变化指标：以智能体在50%成功率下可完成的任务长度（折算人类工作时间）衡量能力；开展广泛检索识别开源替代品；通过Craft套件在12.0天公开开发中产出12个应用仓库作为案例；并结合组织调查自报数据与商业安装量、投资份额等市场指标进行交叉验证。

Result: 2023年以来，领先智能体50%成功率对应的任务时长约每129天翻倍；识别出575个针对特定商业产品的潜在开源替代（部分功能匹配仍需独立验证）；Craft套件12.0天开发产出12个应用仓库，但均为早期版本，完整性与可靠性未经证实；市场证据混杂——购买型软件获美国投资份额上升、可追踪商业安装量增长，但32%的组织调查受访者称至少放弃过一次采购，因可用编程智能体自建功能。

Conclusion: 自报数据无法确定实际被替代的支出规模及自建工具的可靠性；核心结论是一个警示性判断：客户自建能力的提升可能先在个体采购决策中显现，而广泛的采购转移尚未在市场层面可见。作者以基准外推与可观测市场检验考察该可能性，并强调外推属于趋势延伸，并非对客户切换行为的预测。

Abstract: AI coding agents could change whether organizations buy software or build the functionality they need. We focus on organizations and professional buyers whose domain knowledge can help them specify and evaluate tools for their work. We define the software substitution frontier as the functions customers could build and maintain at an acceptable standard for less than buying them. Using public data, we compare growing capacity to build with indicators of market change. Since 2023, the length of benchmark tasks that leading agents can complete at 50 percent success, measured in human working time, has doubled about every 129 days. A broad search identified 575 potential open-source alternatives to selected commercial products, although some functional matches still require independent validation. The Craft suite produced 12 application repositories over 12.0 days of public development, but these remain early versions with unverified completeness and reliability. Market evidence is mixed: purchased software gained US investment share and tracked commercial installations grew. Yet 32 percent of respondents to an organizational survey reported forgoing at least one software purchase because their organization could build the functionality with coding agents. These self-reports do not establish the spending displaced or the reliability of the resulting tools. The gap motivates a warning: customers' capacity to build could affect individual buying decisions before a broad purchasing shift becomes visible. We examine this possibility using benchmark projections and observable market tests. The projections are an extrapolation, not a forecast of customer switching.

</details>


### [245] [TrustedCode: Evaluating Large Language Models for Developing Trusted Code through Automated Program Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30432)
*Muhammad Danial,Muhammad Taimoor Khan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TrustedCode 是一个开源自动化框架，利用 LLM 结合 Why3/WhyML 对 AI 生成的 Python 代码进行形式验证，涵盖类型检查、WhyML 翻译与规约证明，并在 10 个程序上分析了错误来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GitHub 上约 30% 的 Python 贡献由 AI 生成，这类代码本身不可靠；形式验证可建立正确性信任，但现有工具主要面向 Java/C，Python 支持不足，且形式规约编写与验证难度阻碍实用落地。

Method: TrustedCode 使用 LLM 依次完成：(i) Python 代码类型检查；(ii) 经 Why3 框架翻译为 WhyML；(iii) 用 Why3 后端自动定理证明器针对形式规约进行验证。方法通过 LangGraph 集成思维链（chain-of-thought）提示，以识别并缓解 LLM 在规约生成与程序证明中的局限。

Result: 在 10 个不同复杂度的 Python 程序上实验，观察到 50% 的错误源于领域知识缺失，30% 源于语法不准确，其余 20% 属语义与类型相关问题。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了针对 Python 的 LLM 引导形式验证工作流需要定向改进；TrustedCode 以开源形式发布并提供视频演示。

Abstract: Recent reports, such as "Who is using AI to code?", indicate that approximately 30\% of Python code contributions to GitHub are AI-generated. Given the inherent unreliability of this code, formal verification emerges as a promising approach to establish trust in software correctness. Although leveraging large language models (LLMs) for formal verification has garnered increasing attention, practical adoption remains limited due to the challenges in writing formal specifications and verifying program correctness against them. Existing tools predominantly support verification for languages such as Java and C, with minimal support for Python. We present TrustedCode, an automated framework for verifying AI-generated Python code. TrustedCode uses LLMs to (i) type-check Python code, (ii) translate it into WhyML via the Why3 framework, and (iii) verify it against formal specifications using Why3 back-end automated theorem provers. Our method integrates chain-of-thought prompting through LangGraph to identify and address LLM limitations in specification generation and proving the specified programs. To evaluate TrustedCode, we conducted experiments on ten Python programs of varying complexity. Our analysis reveals that 50\% of the observed errors stem from domain knowledge gaps, 30\% from syntactic inaccuracies, and the remaining 20\% from semantic and type-related issues. These findings underscore the importance of targeted improvements in LLM-guided formal verification workflows for Python. TrustedCode is an open source tool available at: \href{github.com/mtaimoorkhan/TrustedCode}{github.com/mtaimoorkhan/TrustedCode}. The video demonstration is available at: \href{youtu.be/Nr5I2g8ec-s}{youtu.be/Nr5I2g8ec-s}

</details>


### [246] [Stale Instruction Files Override the Code in Coding Agents: A One-Sentence Fix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30571)
*Xiaonan Xu,Wenjing Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 编码智能体倾向于遵循项目指令文件（CLAUDE.md / AGENTS.md）而非既有代码，即使两者冲突；在用户级指令文件中加入一句"既有代码优先"的规则，可将遵循代码的比例从 4.2% 提升至 91.4%，并消除代码重写、将测试通过率提升至 100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体会盲目遵循项目的指令文件，即使该文件与既有代码冲突、且智能体已察觉冲突仍照做，这会引入过时约定、破坏代码一致性并导致测试失败。作者希望量化这一行为，并检验一条简单的用户级指令能否让智能体转而以代码为准。

Method: 构建 40 个 Python 项目，每个项目中指令文件要求一种约定（如用 logging），而既有代码使用另一种（如 print）。让 Claude Code 与 Codex CLI 分别为每个项目新增功能，分"有/无"用户级指令中"既有代码优先"这句话两种条件，共 320 次运行。

Result: 无该句时，90.1% 的选择遵循过时指令文件；Claude Code 在 80 次运行中有 79 次指出冲突却仍遵循文件；Codex CLI 在 20/80 次运行中重写既有代码以匹配文件，其中 17 次导致项目自带测试失败。加入该句后，遵循代码的比例由 4.2% 升至 91.4%，重写行为消失，测试通过率由 89.4% 提升至 100%。

Conclusion: 作者将该行为归因于两类信息的输入通道差异：指令文件以"指令"形式进入模型，而代码以"工具输出"形式进入，模型在训练中被要求对后者赋予更低优先级。用户当前即可自行添加该句，智能体与模型开发者也可将同一规则内置于产品中。

Abstract: Purpose: Coding agents follow a project's instruction file, such as CLAUDE.md or AGENTS.md, even when it disagrees with the existing code and even when they notice the disagreement. We measure this behaviour and test whether one sentence in the user's own instruction file makes agents follow the code instead.
  Methods: We built 40 Python projects in which the instruction file asks for one convention while the existing code uses another, for example print instead of logging. Claude Code and Codex CLI added a feature to each project, with and without a sentence in the user-level instruction file that gives the existing code precedence, in 320 runs.
  Results: Without the sentence, 90.1% of the agents' choices followed the stale file. Claude Code pointed out the conflict in 79 of 80 runs and still followed the file. Codex CLI rewrote the existing code to match the file in 20 of 80 runs, and the project's own tests then failed in 17 of them. The sentence raised the share of choices that follow the code from 4.2% to 91.4%, stopped the rewrites and raised the test pass rate from 89.4% to 100%.
  Conclusion: We attribute the behaviour to how agents receive the two sources: the instruction file reaches the model as an instruction and the code as tool output, which models are trained to rank lower. Users can add the sentence today, and makers of agents and models can build the same rule into their products.

</details>


### [247] [From Tokens to Credits to Outcomes: Pricing AI Across Models, Applications, and Cloud Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30701)
*Pratik Gupta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对2023–2026年AI软件定价的可复现研究，基于公开定价页面与文档构建数据集，提出定价原语分类体系，并给出可配置计费平台的实证需求；发现"订阅+用量配额"是AI原生公司主导模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SaaS按席位定价，因边际服务成本近零；而模型推理存在非零边际成本、客户用量差异达数量级，催生预付额度、计量用量、席位加用量混合、结果定价等新模型。这些模型尚未被系统刻画。

Method: 对公开定价制品（定价页与文档）进行可复现研究，设三个队列：Forbes AI 50（2026）全量作为AI原生核心样本、按成文规则纳入的云平台AI服务、冻结的9个特色设计目的性样本。对每个供给的定价原语与信用机制进行编码并标注带日期的一手来源，构建可随论文公开的数据集。

Result: "订阅+用量配额"是AI原生公司中占主导的可观测模型（占可观测AI原生行55%，混合边界敏感性分析下为45–61%）；52个AI原生行中有23行未公开定价；混合产品更适合理解为由打包锚点、配额机制与可计费单位三者组合而成，而非互斥类别。

Conclusion: 贡献三项：（1）带一手来源的定价原语与信用机制编码数据集；（2）在用原语分类体系及其队列分布；（3）面向自动化可配置计费平台的实证需求，含信用账本状态、结果定价归因管线、高定价变更速度的配置支持。所有制品公开，作者雇主被排除在样本外。

Abstract: Between 2023 and 2026, AI changed how software is priced. Traditional SaaS charged per seat because serving one more user cost almost nothing. Model inference has non-zero marginal cost, and usage varies by orders of magnitude across customers, producing prepaid credits, metered usage, hybrid seat-plus-usage models, and outcome pricing that supplement, and in rare cases replace, seat-based pricing. These pricing models have not been systematically characterized. This paper presents a reproducible study of public pricing artifacts - pricing pages and documentation - across three cohorts: the full Forbes AI 50 (2026) as the AI-native core sample, cloud platforms selling AI services included by a documented rule, and a frozen purposive cohort of 9 distinctive designs. It contributes (1) a dataset coding each offering's pricing primitive and credit mechanics with dated primary sources, prepared for public release with the paper, (2) a taxonomy of the primitives in use and their distribution across cohorts, and (3) empirically grounded requirements for automated, configurable billing platforms, including ledger state for credits, attribution pipelines for outcome pricing, and configuration support for high pricing-change velocity. Subscription with usage quota is the dominant observable model among AI-native companies (55% of observable AI-native rows; 45-61% under sensitivity analysis of hybrid boundary cases). 23 of the 52 AI-native rows publish no pricing. Hybrid products are better understood as compositions of packaging anchor, allowance mechanism, and billable unit than as mutually exclusive categories. All artifacts are public; my employer is excluded from the sample.

</details>


### [248] [From Natural Language to Verified C: Verification-Guided Generation with ACSL Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30725)
*Zian Wang,Qin Li,Weisong Sun,Haibin Cai*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: VeriGen-C是一个面向C程序的“验证友好”联合合成框架，从自然语言需求同时生成代码与ACSL规范，使二者作为统一制品共同优化以利于自动验证；在HumanEval-X、FM-Bench、LiveCodeBench Lite上，验证且需求对齐的解率由5.5%→44.5%、44.3%→80.0%、6.5%→58.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助代码生成放大了对自动形式化验证的需求：生成程序不仅要可执行，还需附带机器可检验的正确性证据。现有方法可分别合成功能上合理的代码或看似合理的规范，但未将实现与规范作为面向验证的统一制品进行协同设计，导致验证器难以完成证明义务：缺少或隐含证明锚点、程序结构不利于自动推理、代码级状态与规格级需求事实因独立生成而不一致；即便验证通过，也可能仅建立遗漏需求关键行为的弱规范。

Method: 提出VeriGen-C：一个对形式化验证友好的框架，从自然语言需求联合合成C程序与ACSL规范。代码侧：生成与修复受约束以显式暴露与证明相关的状态、为演绎验证精简控制流、消除妨碍自动证明搜索的实现模式。规范侧：生成与精炼同样受约束，以缓解验证超时并确保与用户需求对齐。

Result: 在HumanEval-X、FM-Bench、LiveCodeBench Lite上，相对接口兼容基线，“通过验证且需求对齐”的解比例分别从5.5%提升至44.5%、44.3%提升至80.0%、6.5%提升至58.7%。

Conclusion: 实现与规范的协同、面向验证约束的联合合成（而非独立生成）是提升自动形式化验证成功率与需求对齐度的关键；VeriGen-C在多个基准上显著优于接口兼容基线。

Abstract: AI-assisted code generation amplifies the demand for automated formal verification. Generated programs must not only be executable but also accompanied by machine-checkable evidence of correctness. Existing approaches can synthesize functionally plausible code or plausible-looking specifications from natural-language prompts; however, they do not explicitly co-design the implementation and specification as a unified artifact optimized for verification. Consequently, automated verifiers often fail to discharge proof obligations. Typical failure modes include missing or implicit proof anchors, program structures that are hostile to automated reasoning, and inconsistencies between code-level state and specification-level requirement facts arising from the independent generation of code and specifications. Even when verification succeeds, it may only establish weak specifications that omit requirement-critical behavior. This paper introduces VeriGen-C, a formal-verification-friendly framework for the joint synthesis of C programs and ACSL specifications from natural-language requirements. Code generation and repair are constrained to expose proof-relevant state, to streamline control flow for deductive verification, and to eliminate implementation patterns that hinder automated proof search. Specification generation and refinement are similarly constrained to mitigate verification timeouts and to ensure alignment with the user requirements. Empirical evaluations on HumanEval-X, FM-Bench, and LiveCodeBench Lite demonstrate that VeriGen-C substantially improves the rate of verified and requirement-aligned solutions relative to an interface-compatible baseline, from 5.5% to 44.5%, from 44.3% to 80.0%, and from 6.5% to 58.7%, respectively.

</details>


### [249] [VLTest: Finding Joint Image-Text Failures in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30794)
*Bin Duan,Jintao Lin,Guowei Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: VLTest是一个黑盒视觉语言模型测试方法，通过过滤可能改变预期答案的变体并采用"四格匹配"（每个变体单独+组合）机制来归因联合失败，在8个VLM上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM已广泛嵌入软件系统，但测试面临两大难题：其一，图像或文本任一输入变异都可能改变预期答案，因此输出变化本身不等于失败（伪失败问题）；其二，某些失败仅在两个输入同时变异时才出现，要将其归因于组合效应必须单独测试每个变异（归因问题）。

Method: 提出黑盒测试方法VLTest：（1）图像变体通过编辑VQGAN隐编码生成，文本变体采用"改写-扩展-剪枝"流水线生成；（2）过滤可能改变预期答案的变体；（3）先单独测试每个变体、再测试组合，使每个联合失败具备四个匹配单元格；（4）由VLM裁判在确认输入保持预期答案后才判定失败。

Result: 在3个任务、8个VLM（含两个商业API模型）上：裁判确认81.7%的候选失败，相对人工标注精确率达88.8%–94.6%；四格确认拒绝了22.5%的联合候选。同等预算下，VLTest生成器发现的已确认失败数为SINVAD和TextFooler的1.8–2.5倍；其联合测试每1000次联合调用发现91–197个可归因联合失败，为基线变体相同配对方式的1.3倍。

Conclusion: VLTest通过变体过滤与四格归因确认机制有效解决了VLM测试中的伪失败与联合失败归因问题，在失败发现效率与归因准确性上显著优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly embedded in software systems. Testing them raises two problems. Mutating either input can change the expected answer, so an output change is not by itself a failure. Some failures appear only when both inputs are mutated; attributing them to the combination requires testing each mutation alone. We present VLTest, a black-box VLM testing approach. It generates image variants by editing VQGAN latent codes and text variants with a rewrite-expand-prune pipeline, filters variants likely to change the expected answer, and tests each variant alone before testing combinations, so every joint failure has four matched cells. A VLM judge confirms a failure only after checking that the input keeps the expected answer. On three tasks with eight VLMs, including two commercial API models, the oracle confirms 81.7% of the candidates, with 88.8% to 94.6% precision against human labels, and four-cell confirmation rejects 22.5% of joint candidates. Under the same budget, VLTest's generators find 1.8 to 2.5 times as many confirmed failures as SINVAD and TextFooler, and its joint tests find 91 to 197 attributed joint failures per 1,000 joint calls, 1.3 times as many as the same pairing applied to the baselines' variants.

</details>


### [250] [What Ethereum Developers Ask For, and Whether They Get It: Ten Years of Ethereum Stack Exchange](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30829)
*Tamer Abdelaziz*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究分析了2016–2025年Ethereum Stack Exchange全部54,637个提问，用主题管线标注出22个主题，发现需求集中于合约编写/部署/调试，且"谁问"比"问什么"更能解释问题被解决与否：回复率下降9.5–18.6个百分点，获接受答案率下降16.2–24.5个百分点，而询问者声望差异带来47个百分点跨度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有对Ethereum开发者困难的理解来自问卷与访谈，这类数据只记录开发者在被问及特定主题时的说法（有提示、受引导）；而ESE记录的是开发者自发提出的问题以及是否被解决，能提供无提示、完整的真实需求与供给画像，弥补调查法的偏差。

Method: 以2016年1月至2025年12月ESE全部54,637个问题为全量语料；用基于prompt的主题管线打标签，预设8个Ethereum专属主题并向新主题开放，共得22个主题；由三家模型供应商组成的委员会审核标签，对所有争议样本进行人工裁决，准确率87.9%–93.5%；因语料为全量记录，比率不含抽样误差；用五种估计量衡量十年间变化，并以早期主题构成重算后期接受率做构成效应分解（9.40→9.32个百分点）。

Result: 需求集中在合约编写、部署与调试；工具链为第二大主题且在增长，安全、共识、扩展性较少。供给减弱且不均衡：回答仍会出现，但问题关闭（达成解决）下降更多。任回答率下降9.5–18.6个百分点，而已获回答问题中达成接受答案的比例下降16.2–24.5个百分点。主题构成几乎不解释下降。询问者身份的解释力远强于问题内容：接受率从声望<10的19.8%到≥10k的66.8%，跨度47个百分点且每年都存在梯度，而主题间跨度仅17。询问者转向新用户可解释接受率下降的最多59%；询问者可控制的七项属性中最大一项伴随约5个百分点差异。

Conclusion: ESE的完整记录显示支持缺口集中在工具链领域与新加入的开发者身上；平台问题解决能力的下滑主要不是主题变化所致，而是询问者构成（尤其是新手比例上升）与供给端衰减所致。

Abstract: The Ethereum community learns where developers struggle through surveys and interviews, which record what developers say when asked about a chosen theme. Ethereum Stack Exchange (ESE) records what they ask for unprompted, and whether anyone resolves it. We study every question posted to ESE from Jan 2016 to Dec 2025, 54,637 in all, and label each with a prompt-based topic pipeline seeded with eight Ethereum-specific topics and open to new ones (22 topics in all). A three-provider committee of language models screens the labels, and manual adjudication of every disputed case puts their accuracy at 87.9 to 93.5%. Because the corpus is the complete record, no rate carries sampling error. We ask what developers ask for help with, whether the venue still resolves it, and, where it does not, whether the questions changed or their resolution did.
  Demand is concentrated on writing, deploying, and debugging contracts; the toolchain is the second-largest topic and growing, while security, consensus, and scaling are thin. Supply has weakened, and unevenly: answers still arrive, closure falls further. Across five estimators of the decade, the share of questions drawing any answer fell by 9.5 to 18.6 percentage points, but among answered questions the share reaching an accepted answer fell by 16.2 to 24.5. Rebuilding the later acceptance rate at the earlier topic mix moves the fall only from 9.40 to 9.32 points, so the topic mix does not explain it. Who asks separates outcomes far more sharply than what is asked: acceptance runs from 19.8% for askers below 10 reputation to 66.8% at 10k and above, a 47- point spread with a gradient in every year, against 17 across topics, and a shift toward newer askers accounts for up to 59% of the acceptance fall. The largest of seven properties an asker controls accompanies 5 points. The record places the support gap in the toolchain and among newcomers.

</details>


### [251] [Separate Field Scoring Protects Unused Tools When Agents Pool Tool-Use Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30867)
*Abhijit Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 在共享工具目录下，把工具使用示例加入检索索引作为额外条目，会提升已用工具的 Hit@5 但显著损害未使用（held-out）工具；作者提出 SYNAPSE 将使用情况作为独立字段打分，能在三个目录和两种嵌入器上保护 held-out 工具的召回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体共享工具目录时，各自积累的“哪个工具适合哪类请求”的使用经验本可汇聚复用，但需要明确这种汇聚对工具检索的真实影响：既要量化对已有使用记录工具的增益，也要区分对无任何使用记录的 held-out 工具的副作用，从而判断应如何把使用信号纳入检索。

Method: 将使用示例作为额外条目加入索引，统计已用工具与 held-out 工具的 Hit@5；设置等量等长但填充其他工具文本的对照条目以分离“条目数量/长度”效应；提出 SYNAPSE，把使用情况作为独立字段评分，并与为有使用记录的工具保留配额槽位的 router 对比；在 StableToolBench、TOUCAN、ToolRet 三个目录、两种嵌入器上评测，并用常量分数偏移与十智能体模拟汇聚做消融。

Result: 加入使用示例使已用工具 Hit@5 上升、held-out 工具下降，三个目录中两个降幅很大；填充他工具文本的对照使 held-out Hit@5 降幅相近但几乎不产生增益。StableToolBench 上 SYNAPSE 的 held-out 金标工具进入前五的比例为 0.785，高于配额式 router 的 0.683，且该领先在三个目录、两种嵌入器上均成立；将使用文本替换为他工具文本或改用常量分数偏移时，held-out 保护大多仍存在，而已用工具的增益则依赖使用文本确实对应该工具。TOUCAN（两种嵌入器）与 ToolRet（一种嵌入器）上 router 在带标注查询上保留更多增益；十个模拟智能体分摊使用记录与单一智能体持有同等记录效果一致。

Conclusion: 使用示例直接入索引会让已用工具挤占 held-out 工具的排名空间，增益部分来自条目数量而非使用内容；将使用作为独立字段评分（SYNAPSE）能稳定保护 held-out 工具，且其优势可跨目录与嵌入器泛化。最优方案取决于工作负载：若更看重带标注查询上的增益，配额式 router 可能更好；汇聚式共享对多智能体场景无额外损耗，但真正增益要求使用记录与被评工具正确对应。

Abstract: Agents that call tools from a shared catalog can pool what they learn about which tool serves which request. We measure what pooled usage examples do to tool retrieval, reporting separately the held-out tools, for which no agent has usage, and all other tools. Adding usage examples to the index as extra entries for used tools raises Hit@5 (whether a gold tool is in the top five) for those tools and lowers it for held-out tools, by a large margin on two of three catalogs and with both embedders we tested. Giving used tools the same number of equally long entries filled with other tools' text lowers held-out Hit@5 about as much, but gives little or none of the gain. We call our method of scoring usage as its own field SYNAPSE. On StableToolBench, the held-out gold tool appears in its top five more often than with a router that reserves slots for usage (0.785 against 0.683), and this held-out lead holds on all three catalogs with both embedders. This protection persisted when we replaced each tool's usage with other tools' text, and a constant score offset for tools with usage gives similar held-out Hit@5 in most settings. The gain for tools with usage, in contrast, needs usage about the right tool. The router keeps more of the gain on labeled queries on TOUCAN with both embedders and on ToolRet with one, so the better choice depends on the workload. Splitting the usage among ten simulated agents shows that pooling helps exactly as much as giving one agent the same records.

</details>


### [252] [ATLAS: A Multi-Agent Framework for Lifting Binary to Canonical Compiler IR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30877)
*Xiaoao Zhu,Ni Zhang,Jie Ren,Zhiqiang Li,Jie Zheng,Zhanyong Tang,Zheng Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ATLAS：多智能体框架，从去符号、重度优化的二进制反汇编中重建形式合法且规范的优化前LLVM IR，通过检索增强的初稿生成与验证驱动的协作修复闭环，在ARM/x86-64/RISC-V上达到91.8%可重执行率与89.4% CFG相似度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无源码场景下的下游安全分析与遗留软件迁移需要把剥离符号、高度优化的二进制提升到规范的、优化前的LLVM IR。但激进编译优化重写了控制流与数据流、移除了结构元数据，并引入类型擦除的低层惯用法，导致现有lifter产出畸形或语义偏离的IR。

Method: 两部分：①结构感知的区域级检索，利用伪探针（pseudo-probe）归属将优化后指令与规范IR范例对齐；②协作式精炼循环——Lift智能体基于检索范例生成初始IR草案，Advisor智能体解读LLVM verifier/编译诊断与静态检查并提出局部修复动作，Validation智能体应用编辑、重跑工具链验证及（可得时）运行时检查并将诊断反馈给Advisor，迭代至IR通过验证且行为一致或达到预算上限。

Result: 在HumanEval-Decompile与MBPP上，于LLVM IR提升任务上优于7个基线，跨ARM、x86-64、RISC-V取得91.8%可重执行率与89.4% CFG相似度；相比Binary→C→IR流水线，在指针流精度与下游可分析性上更优。

Conclusion: 通过恢复良构的、以机器为中心的表示，ATLAS支持实用的跨ISA重编译与可验证的纯二进制迁移工作流。

Abstract: Lifting stripped, heavily optimized binaries to a canonical, pre-optimization LLVM intermediate representation (IR) is essential for downstream security analysis and legacy software migration when source code is unavailable. However, aggressive compiler optimizations rewrite control and data flow, remove structural metadata, and introduce type-erased low-level idioms, causing current lifters to produce malformed or semantically divergent IR. We present ATLAS, a multi-agent framework designed to reconstruct formally valid and canonical LLVM IR from optimized disassembly.ATLAS combines (i) Structure-aware, region-level retrieval, which utilizes pseudo-probe attribution to align optimized instructions with canonical IR exemplars, and (ii) a collaborative refinement loop that closes the gap between probabilistic generation and deterministic compiler constraints. Concretely, a Lift agent generates an initial IR draft conditioned on retrieved exemplars, an Advisor agent interprets LLVM verifier/compilation diagnostics and static checks to propose localized repair actions, and a Validation agent applies the edits, re-runs toolchain verification and (when available) runtime checks, and feeds the resulting diagnostics back to the Advisor. The agents iterate until the IR is verifier-clean and behaviorally consistent, or a budget limit is reached. On HumanEval-Decompile and MBPP, ATLAS outperforms seven baselines on lifting LLVM IR, achieving 91.8% re-executability and 89.4% CFG similarity across ARM, x86-64, and RISC-V binaries. Moreover, our evaluation demonstrates that ATLAS also outperforms the Binary to C to IR pipelines in pointer-flow precision and downstream analyzability. By recovering a well-formed, machine-centric representation, ATLAS enables practical cross-ISA recompilation and verifiable binary-only migration workflows.

</details>


### [253] [When Code Gets Cheap, Evaluation Gets Scarce: An Experience Report on AI-Assisted Agile Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30916)
*Barbara Weber,Olaf Zimmermann,Marco Franceschetti*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该文报告单一开发者在不手写代码的前提下，于三个工作日内端到端构建并部署一个合规关键、人在回路（human-in-the-loop）的考试评分系统，并提出核心论断：AI 对软件工程的改变主要不在"写代码更快"，而在"稀缺性转移"——编码变得充裕，可靠评估（evaluation）成为绑定约束，工程判断是第二稀缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于编码助手的叙述多止于"加速代码产出"，缺乏对端到端构建与真实部署全过程的考察。作者希望以合规关键的评分系统为极端场景，检验当代码生成成本极低时，真正的瓶颈究竟落在何处。

Method: 单开发者案例研究（作者同时是开发者、最终用户与领域专家，形成"三重合一"）。采用带门禁（gated）、可追溯的构建流程，从利益相关者画像（stakeholder persona）到经审计的行为；仅用三天完成初版并可对真实队列评分。随后观察测试套件漏检的缺陷、部署中暴露的假设偏差，以及AI助手跨多步执行时绕过流程检查点的行为；另以一个第二项目和一份独立的单专家案例作佐证。

Result: 多个缺陷通过了测试套件，集中在被mock的边界（浏览器与语言模型API）或仅在实际使用中出现的场景；部署暴露了本地测试遗漏的假设。AI助手在多步任务中曾绕过流程检查点，直到把检查点改造为可强制执行的门禁才被约束。即便在评估成本已被压到最低的"三重合一"条件下，评估仍构成进度约束。

Conclusion: 提出五条经验：(1) 运行带门禁、可追溯的构建流程；(2) 明确哪些是可先验规定的、哪些须靠运行中原型发现；(3) 在真实环境中验证；(4) 门禁对象应是助手本身，而非仅其产出；(5) 记录决策及其假设，以及助手会遗忘的流程状态。作者指出，该案例对"评估稀缺"是最严苛的检验，若评估需额外人员或专业知识，迭代收益可能缩小；第二项目与独立单专家案例呈现相似模式。

Abstract: Coding assistants are widely reported to speed up code production; most accounts stop there. We report the end-to-end construction and deployment of a compliance-critical, human-in-the-loop exam-grading system, built by a single developer who wrote no code by hand, protected by a gated process from stakeholder persona to audited behavior. The initial system was built in three working days and could grade a real cohort. We draw one claim from this experience: AI changes software engineering less by making programming faster than by changing what is scarce. Writing code becomes abundant. Reliable evaluation-knowing whether a result is correct and meets the actual need-remains necessary at every iteration and becomes the binding constraint. Engineering judgment remains a second scarcity. Several defects passed the test suite: they sat at the boundaries the tests had mocked, the browser and the language-model API, or appeared only in real use. Deployment exposed assumptions that local tests had missed. Moreover, an AI assistant acting across many steps bypassed process checkpoints until we made them enforceable gates. All of this was observed in a triple union: the developer was also the end user and domain expert, making expert judgment immediately available and evaluation as cheap as it can be. This favors the iteration gains, which may shrink where evaluation requires additional people or expertise, but makes the case a hard test for evaluation scarcity: evaluation still constrained progress. We draw five lessons: run a gated, traceable construction process; specify what is knowable and discover the rest against a running prototype; validate in the real environment; gate the assistant, not only the work; record decisions with their assumptions and the process state the assistant forgets. A second project showed similar patterns; an independent single-expert case corroborates the shift.

</details>


### [254] [REGATTA: A Cross-Domain Measurement Framework and Fault-Injection Harness for Enterprise AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30924)
*Luong Tuan Thanh,Abhijit Sanyal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 REGATTA 框架，通过"两阶段测试+保证"测量企业 AI 智能体的评测套件：一是指向法规来源的 125 项清单，二是 25 项故障语料库及评分工具，并对 180 个生成套件做四项测量诊断；结果显示来源审计存在大量缺陷、设计阶段与执行阶段间一致性未通过预设不等式，但盲审支持判定协议的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业 AI 智能体评测缺乏关于两方面证据：测试套件能否暴露指定故障，以及其参照规则是否可信。作者希望建立可测量的框架来同时评估"故障检测能力"与"规则来源质量"，而非直接把评测结果等同于法律合规。

Method: 构建三件工具：(1) 125 项带来源引用的清单，覆盖美国与越南五个"行业×司法辖区"单元；(2) 25 项故障语料库，分五类；(3) 可在设计与执行两阶段对套件级故障检测打分的 harness。在三个 LLM 生成器产生的 180 个套件（5,400 个场景）上运行四项测量诊断：来源审计、条目覆盖度、故障画像稳定性、阶段间一致性；并以当前模型做美国保险业重测、重复执行变异对照和专家盲审（κ 一致性）验证判定协议。

Result: 来源审计发现 24 项条目误述现行规则、46 项额外引用缺陷、31 项仅有非约束性/不可识别/不可核验来源，仅 24 项经核实无瑕疵；且只有初始被实质标记的条目经作者复核，全部干净条目仅经 AI 复核。条目覆盖度不均；执行阶段故障画像跨生成器稳定；历史阶段在 66%–69% 的"故障–套件"对上一致。当前模型美国保险重测中阶段分歧超过重复执行变异，但阶段一致率 .536 低于合并机会水平 .549，未通过 V3 下界不等式。单专家盲审支持设计阶段判定协议（κ=.72，95% 区间 [.52,.88]），40 次当前模型执行探针给出 κ=.75。工具、审计与可复用代码均已开源。

Conclusion: 结果描述的是"按书面定义的工具"本身，而非法律合规性。REGATTA 提供了可复用的测量与审计基础设施，但来源引用质量是主要短板，且设计阶段与执行阶段判定的一致性尚不足以支持阶段等价假设，需进一步改进判定协议与来源核验流程。

Abstract: Enterprise AI-agent evaluation needs evidence about whether test suites expose specified faults and whether their reference rules are trustworthy. We introduce REGATTA (Regulatory Evaluation of Agents via Two-stage Testing and Assurance), a measurement framework with three instruments. A 125-item source-cited checklist spans five industry-by-jurisdiction cells across the United States and Vietnam. A 25-fault corpus covers five categories, and a harness scores suite-level fault detection at the design and execution stages. We examine four measurement diagnostics on 180 generated suites (5,400 scenarios) from three LLM generators. A source audit finds 24 items misstating the rule in force, 46 additional citation defects, 31 items with only non-binding, unidentifiable, or unverifiable sources, and 24 verified clean. Only initially substance-flagged items received author review; all 24 clean rows received AI review only. Results therefore describe the instruments as written, not legal compliance. Item coverage varies, execution-stage fault profiles are stable across generators, and the historical stages agree on $66$--$69\%$ of fault--suite pairs. A current-model US-insurance retest finds stage disagreement above repeated-execution variation, but stage agreement $.536$ falls below pooled chance $.549$, failing V3's lower inequality. Blind adjudication by one expert supports the design-stage judging protocol ($κ=.72$, 95% interval $[.52,.88]$); 40 current-model execution probes give $κ=.75$. The instruments, audit, and reusable code are released.

</details>


### [255] [Tracing Hallucinations in LLM Reasoning for UML Class Diagram Generation: An Empirical Taxonomy and Mitigation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30946)
*Mojdeh Rahmanian,Foutse Khomh,Ashkan Sami,Yanchao Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文构建了面向LLM从需求生成UML类图过程的幻觉分类法（11个子类型、3个维度），基于推理轨迹进行标注与统计，并开展提示词约束的缓解实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多只考察最终类图，未分析推理型LLM的中间轨迹，无法定位幻觉产生的环节。作者希望将幻觉在生成过程中"定位"，以支持针对性的提示词缓解。

Method: 从60条人工标注的DeepSeek-R1推理轨迹中归纳出三维度（Prompt Faithfulness、Code Faithfulness、Reasoning Quality）共11个幻觉子类型的经验性分类法；通过双LLM标注流水线扩展到另外120条DeepSeek-R1轨迹，并在20条DeepSeek-V4-Pro轨迹上检验；分析轨迹长度、需求复杂度与幻觉频次的关系；在13个Golden UML ModelSet案例、三个Qwen3模型上进行提示词约束缓解实验。

Result: DeepSeek-R1在两个数据集上均以Code Faithfulness最为频发，而DeepSeek-V4-Pro以Prompt Faithfulness为主，显示失败模式具有模型依赖性。人工标注轨迹中，轨迹长度与幻觉频次无显著单调关联；需求复杂度与幻觉频次呈负相关，尤其对Prompt Faithfulness错误。缓解实验中，加入Prompt Faithfulness与Reasoning Quality约束使汇总平均F1提升4.0个百分点，而事后Code Faithfulness审查无一致额外收益；关系建模始终得分最低。

Conclusion: 在生成过程中定位幻觉可指导有针对性的提示词缓解；但结论是否能推广到DeepSeek与Qwen3以外的模型族仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate UML class diagrams from natural language requirements, but hallucinations may compromise the correctness and completeness of the resulting diagrams. Existing evaluations mostly assess the final diagram, without examining the intermediate traces of reasoning-capable LLMs, which can reveal where these hallucinations arise. We develop an empirical taxonomy of eleven hallucination sub-types organised into three dimensions: Prompt Faithfulness (deviations between requirements and reasoning), Code Faithfulness (discrepancies between reasoning and generated PlantUML code), and Reasoning Quality (problems within the reasoning). The taxonomy was derived from 60 manually annotated DeepSeek-R1 traces, applied to 120 further DeepSeek-R1 traces through a dual-LLM annotation pipeline, and examined on 20 DeepSeek-V4-Pro traces. Code Faithfulness was the most frequent dimension for DeepSeek-R1 in both datasets, whereas Prompt Faithfulness dominated for DeepSeek-V4-Pro, suggesting model-dependent failure patterns. In the manually annotated traces, reasoning-trace length showed no significant monotonic association with hallucination frequency, whereas requirement complexity was negatively associated with it, particularly for Prompt Faithfulness errors. In a mitigation experiment on 13 Golden UML ModelSet cases with three Qwen3-based models, adding Prompt Faithfulness and Reasoning Quality constraints to the generation prompt raised the pooled mean F1 score by 4.0 percentage points, whereas a post-hoc Code Faithfulness review provided no consistent further benefit. Relationship modelling remained the lowest-scoring element throughout. These results suggest that localising hallucinations within the generation process can inform targeted, prompt-based mitigation. Whether these findings generalise beyond the DeepSeek and Qwen3 model families remains to be tested.

</details>


### [256] [ProductWebBench: Evaluating Website Continuity Beyond Task Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31118)
*Jiajun Wu,Jian Yang,Yaxin Du,Shukai Liu,Linzheng Chai,Tuney Zheng,Wei Zhang,Haitao Yang,Yan Bai,Yihang Lou,Xianglong Liu,Bryan Dai*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ProductWebBench，一个在真实前端仓库上同时评估"目标功能是否建成"（requested target）与"网站连续性"（website continuity）的基准，包含 320 个 Change 任务与 80 个长程 Build 任务。在 8,737 次运行中发现：即使所有目标检查通过，仍有 28.2% 的运行破坏连续性，18.3% 使已验证状态回退，交互层面在静态 DOM 已通过的运行中另有 29.5% 失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前端编码智能体的评测只看目标是否被构建，忽视了真实工程改动的第二项义务——站点其余部分必须继续正常工作（website continuity）。缺少能同时刻画"新增正确"与"不破坏既有行为"的评测手段。

Method: 构建 ProductWebBench：基于活动中的前端仓库，含 320 个 Change 任务（在既有代码上改动）与 80 个长程 Build 任务；每个任务预先冻结仓库快照、分阶段需求、浏览器证据与确定性检查，任务仅在参考解通过全部检查且"貌似合理但错误"的解失败后才入库。评测中由模拟用户逐阶段揭示需求，且此前通过的检查在整轮运行中持续保持监控。

Result: 13 个模型共 8,737 次运行显示两项指标相互分离：通过全部 requested-work 检查的 Change 运行中，28.2% 仍违反连续性检查（空白路由、横向溢出、控制台报错），10.8% 将已通过的前端状态重新弄坏；在同时具备两种读数的 586 次运行中，18.3% 被捕获为破坏了已验证状态。静态 DOM 已通过的运行中，交互层面仍有 29.5% 失败。

Conclusion: 前端编码智能体的评测应从"仅目标"转向"补丁落入的活动系统"，ProductWebBench 将评分对象从单一目标扩展为真实运行系统的稳定性，揭示了连续性、状态回退与交互层面的系统性盲区。

Abstract: Frontend coding agents are usually graded on one question: did the requested target get built. Real engineering changes carry a second obligation, because the rest of the site has to keep working. We call that second obligation website continuity, and introduce ProductWebBench, a benchmark that scores both on living frontend repositories: 320 Change tasks over existing code and 80 long-horizon Build tasks. Every task freezes a repository snapshot, staged requirements, browser evidence and deterministic checks before evaluation, and enters only once a reference passes every check and plausible-wrong ones fail. A simulated user reveals the requirements one stage at a time, and every check cleared earlier stays armed for the rest of the run. Across 8,737 runs from 13 models the two come apart: of the Change runs passing every requested-work check, 28.2% still fail a continuity check -- a blank route, horizontal overflow, console errors -- and 10.8% push a browser state they had already passed back to failing. Because the same checks also run on the untouched repository, each regression carries exact provenance, not a post-hoc label: on the 586 runs where both readings exist, 18.3% are caught breaking a verified state. Interaction is a further blind spot, failing in 29.5% of runs whose static DOM already passes. ProductWebBench therefore scores a patch against the living system it lands in, not the target alone.

</details>


### [257] [Beyond Accuracy: Disentangling Decomposition from Typed-State Quality Assurance in Multi-Agent LLM Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31280)
*Maria Gabriela Alves Zuppardo,Vinicius Cardoso Garcia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文研究多智能体LLM流水线的性能增益归因问题：以需求工程中的功能/非功能需求分类与MoSCoW优先级排序为任务，比较单次调用、两次调用（复用专门提示但传递未验证中间结果）、Pydantic类型化状态流水线三种架构，共18组条件（PROMISE NFR+ 625条需求、3个本地小模型、2种语言）。结论是F1增益主要来自两次调用加专门提示（最高+0.075），类型化状态仅带来-0.003至+0.013的微小差异，且仅在6组对比中的1组显著；类型化流水线能在智能体边界拦截越界输出，未验证变体则在后续阶段失败并丢弃需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM流水线常被认为在软件工程任务中带来增益，但增益究竟源于智能体之间的类型化契约（typed contract），还是源于任务分解所促成的专门提示（specialized prompts），此前鲜有清晰区分。作者希望在需求工程场景中对这一归因问题进行受控分析。

Method: 在需求工程任务上对比三种架构：1）单次调用基线；2）两次调用变体，复用流水线的专门提示但传递未经校验的中间结果；3）带Pydantic校验类型化状态的完整流水线。任务为将需求分类为功能性/非功能性并进行MoSCoW优先级排序。实验设18组条件，覆盖PROMISE NFR+数据集（625条需求）、3个本地部署的小语言模型和2种语言。实现与复现包已存档于Zenodo（DOI: 10.5281/zenodo.23380444）。

Result: 相对基线的F1增益与"两次调用＋专门提示"变体相关（最高+0.075）；类型化状态仅带来-0.003至+0.013的小幅差异，且在6组对比中仅1组显著。准确性之外，类型化流水线能在智能体边界处拦截模型输出的越界值，而未校验变体则在更后阶段失败并丢弃该需求；类型化流水线还表现出模型间MoSCoW一致性小幅但可复现的提升，但其成因无法由当前设计分离。

Conclusion: 该研究属关联性分析：对比条件同时捆绑了提示、调用次数与分阶段上下文，且结果仅来自单一数据集上的三个7–8B模型。因此不能断言类型化契约本身带来准确性增益，增益更可能来自分解所赋予的专门提示；类型化状态的价值主要体现在错误边界处的前置拦截与鲁棒性。后续需解耦各因素并扩展到更大模型与多数据集以确认因果。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM pipelines are often credited with gains in software engineering tasks, but it is rarely clear whether a gain stems from the typed contract between agents or from the specialized prompts that decomposition enables. We study this attribution problem in Requirements Engineering, comparing three architectures for classifying requirements as functional or non-functional and then prioritizing them with MoSCoW: a single-call baseline; a two-call variant that reuses the pipeline's specialized prompts but passes an unvalidated intermediate; and a pipeline with a Pydantic-validated typed state. The 18 conditions cover PROMISE NFR+ (625 requirements), three locally hosted small language models, and two languages. The F1 gain over the baseline is associated with the two-call, specialized-prompt variant (up to +0.075), whereas the typed state adds a small difference (-0.003 to +0.013) that is significant in one of six comparisons. Beyond accuracy, the typed pipeline contains out-of-range model outputs at the agent boundary, while the unvalidated variant fails later and drops the requirement, and it shows a small, reproducible increase in inter-model MoSCoW consistency whose cause our design cannot isolate. The study is associative: the contrasts bundle prompts, call count, and staged context, and the results come from three 7--8B models on one dataset. The implementation and replication package are archived on Zenodo (https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.23380444).

</details>


### [258] [Superintelligent Software Engineering: Governance That Scales with Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31306)
*Mohammad Mehdi Gholinejad*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出"超级智能软件工程"概念：能力超越人类工程师的智能体必须以同等规模化的治理（闸门、契约、决策、知识衰减控制）才能产出更好的软件；并提出 OSTEON 纪律框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究以提升智能体能力为目标，但作者认为仅靠能力提升不能带来更好的软件工程，因为项目正确性依赖代码之外未承载的决策与契约、知识会随时间衰减，且当智能体产出速度超过评审者阅读速度时，基于陈旧知识的变更更容易逃逸审查。

Method: 论证基于三点主张并构建一个简单分析模型（评审容量固定后，逃逸变更随吞吐量线性增长，而阅读捕获比例趋近于零；治理可降低斜率，能力不能）；对 452 个最高星标公开 GitHub 仓库做探索性快照，统计 agent 上下文文件覆盖情况与路径引用失效比例；随后提出 OSTEON 纪律：受治理的决策与契约知识库、每次变更的机械校验、每次会话启动时检查知识衰减、规划设闸门、会话记录所学但不写入规则。

Result: 48.67%（95% CI 44.1–53.3）的仓库在根目录或 .github 下存在 agent 上下文文件；在中位数的具名路径文件中，按不同引用解读方式，9.5%–22% 的路径无法解析。作者明确未报告 OSTEON 的实测效果。

Conclusion: 治理必须随能力共同扩展；能力本身无法解决审查逃逸与知识衰减问题。OSTEON 作为设计性提案以 DOI 发布，其机制主张被标注为观点、作者实践启发式标注为设计理由，并给出可证伪该论点的研究问题。

Abstract: Software agents are being trained to exceed human engineers; we argue that capability alone does not produce better software engineering. We define superintelligent software engineering as the discipline of building and evolving software with agents whose capability exceeds that of human engineers, in which governance (gates, contracts, decisions and decay control) scales with capability. The argument rests on three claims. First, correctness in a project rests on decisions and contracts that the code does not carry. Second, knowledge decays. Third, once an agent outpaces its reviewers, reading catches a shrinking share of the changes built on stale knowledge. A simple model shows that, past a fixed review capacity, escaping changes grow linearly with throughput while the share that reading catches falls toward zero; governance can lower the slope, capability alone cannot. An exploratory snapshot of 452 of the most-starred public GitHub repositories finds a root-level or .github agent context file in 48.67% (95% confidence interval 44.1 to 53.3); in the median file that names repository paths, 9.5% to 22% of those paths do not resolve, depending on how a reference is read. We then present OSTEON, a discipline for the regime, released with a DOI (10.5281/zenodo.23436020): each project gets a governed knowledge repository of decisions and contracts, every change is checked mechanically against it, and the knowledge itself is checked for decay at every session start; planning is gated, and sessions record what they learn without writing rules. We report no measured effect of OSTEON, mark claims about its mechanisms as opinion and the author's practice heuristics as design rationale, and state the questions that would falsify the thesis.

</details>


### [259] [Typed Template Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31342)
*Lu Maltsis,Dominik Winterer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 Typed Template Fuzzing (TTF)：用 LLM 在预处理阶段把种子转换为"类型化模板"，从而在模糊测试期间无需语言解析器、类型检查器或额外 LLM 查询即可实现语义感知的变异测试。实现工具 Typos，在 21 种输入语言上平均 96% 变异体有效，六个月报告 135 个 bug（128 个此前未知）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂输入的模糊测试长期困难：传统方法需程序化编码语言约束（工程量大）或依赖用户声明语法与约束；现有 LLM 模糊器亦有缺陷——直接让 LLM 生成测试会降低吞吐、LLM 出错时产生无效输入；或依赖语言专用解析器、静态分析与 AST 操作库，难以跨语言通用。

Method: TTF 将 LLM 限制在预处理环节：把种子转换为类型化模板（typed templates），模板中标注语法结构与类型信息；测试循环中基于模板做语义感知的变异式模糊测试，无需语言专用解析器、类型检查器或在线 LLM 调用。同时利用种子与模板的对应关系来修复 LLM 生成时的错误。实现为工具 Typos。

Result: 在 21 种输入语言（含 C、C++、Go、Rust、SystemVerilog、Scala、Java 及 PyTorch、Qiskit 等库）上评估；六个月模糊测试活动中报告 135 个 bug，其中 128 个为先前未知；平均 96% 的变异体有效，接近领域专用模糊器的有效率；并显示相较 SOTA 的优势。

Conclusion: 将 LLM 使用前移至预处理阶段、以类型化模板承载语义信息，是兼顾通用性、吞吐与输入有效性的可行路线；Typos 在多语言与真实库上验证了该方法的实用价值与相对现有方法的优越性。

Abstract: Fuzzing software with complex inputs has been a longstanding challenge. Traditionally, fuzzers either programmatically encode language constraints, requiring significant engineering effort, or rely on users to declare grammars and constraints. Recent LLM-based fuzzers address these drawbacks but introduce new issues. Some use LLMs for test generation, sacrificing throughput and producing invalid inputs when the LLM makes mistakes. Others rely on language-specific parsers, static analysis, and AST manipulation libraries. We introduce Typed Template Fuzzing (TTF), a lightweight LLM-based fuzzing methodology. The key idea is to use LLMs in a preprocessing step to transform seeds into typed templates. The typed templates then enable semantically aware, mutation-based fuzzing without language-specific parsers, type checkers, or further LLM queries during fuzzing. By exploiting the correspondence between seeds and templates, TTF can moreover repair LLM mistakes. We have implemented TTF in our tool Typos, which we evaluate across 21 input languages, including C, C++, Go, Rust, SystemVerilog, Scala, and Java, as well as libraries such as PyTorch and Qiskit. In a six-month fuzzing campaign, we reported 135 bugs, 128 of which were previously unknown. Perhaps most notably, on average 96% of Typos's mutants are valid, coming close to the validity rates of domain-specific fuzzers. Our evaluation further shows Typos's benefits over the state of the art.

</details>


### [260] [Automating Feature Flag Cleanup at Scale with Multi-Agent LLM Systems: An Industrial Experience at DoorDash](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31351)
*Atharv Chandratre,Jai Datar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一套端到端、基于大语言模型（LLM）的多智能体功能开关清理系统，集成 MCP 查询线上开关上线数据、Jira 工单处理与自动创建 PR，在 50 个陈旧开关上为 45 个（90%）生成了工程师认可的移除 PR，单次清理平均成本 4.79 美元、耗时 13.8 分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功能开关在特性全量上线后即成为技术债，可能误启用休眠代码路径而带来运维风险。DoorDash 现有 56,000+ 开关分布于 623 个仓库，截至 2026 年 5 月已有 1,659 个陈旧开关，人工清理每个需 1–2 小时；既有基于规则的清理工具在其代码库中效果不足。

Method: 构建端到端 LLM 驱动的自动化清理系统：通过 MCP（Model Context Protocol）查询实时开关上线（rollout）数据，接入 Jira 工单处理流程，并由多智能体协作完成代码定位、开关移除与端到端 Pull Request 自动创建。

Result: 在 50 个陈旧开关上评估，系统为 45 个（90%）成功产出获工程师批准的开关移除 PR；平均每次清理成本 4.79 美元、耗时 13.8 分钟。基于成功率、单次成本与时间指标界定了系统的质量与可靠性边界。

Conclusion: 该方案验证了 LLM 多智能体系统在大规模开关清理中的运维可行性，并总结了系统架构设计中的实践经验，为开发同类多智能体系统提供可操作指导。

Abstract: Feature flags are runtime configuration mechanisms used for gradual feature rollouts, experimentation, and kill switches. Once a feature is fully rolled out, the flag and its gated code become technical debt and can introduce operational risk through inadvertently enabling dormant code paths. At DoorDash, over 56,000 feature flags span 623 repositories, with 1,659 currently stale as of May 2026, with internal estimates of one to two hours per manual cleanup. Existing rule-based cleanup tools proved insufficient in DoorDash's codebases. Our contribution is an end-to-end automated LLM-based feature flag cleanup system that integrates querying live feature flag rollout data using MCP, Jira ticket processing, and end-to-end pull request creation. Our system successfully produced engineer-approved flag removal PRs for 45 (90%) of the 50 stale flags it was evaluated on, proving the system's operational viability at scale with each cleanup averaging only $4.79 and 13.8 minutes. We evaluated the system's quality and reliability boundary based on success rates, cost per cleanup and time metrics. Furthermore, we discuss our lessons learned when architecting this system, to provide practical guidance for developing similar multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [261] [Procrustes-Bering-Sheaft: Automated Model Discovery and Virtual Crash Tests for Microservices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31444)
*Anatoly A. Krasnovsky*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Procrustes-Bering-Sheaft 工具链自动化微服务轻量模型发现与虚拟崩溃测试，通过蒙特卡洛采样估计操作可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为微服务提供可自动化、可复用、可集成的轻量模型发现与虚拟崩溃测试手段，弥补早期基于图的方法在契约、容错、基线与组件重要性等方面能力不足。

Method: 三个独立命令行工具分工：Procrustes 检查部署与追踪证据；Bering 发现依赖并纳入副本声明；Sheaft 评估声明的操作成功条件。工具间交换可复用、带版本的工件；方法扩展包括强制调用契约、容错扫描、基线比较、组件重要性分析与 CI/CD 门禁。

Result: 在一个 HTTP 操作上演示流程，六种受控执行状态暴露了可达性与实测成功之间的两处不一致，而显式必需调用谓词全部匹配；两个建模的单副本新增方案决策不同：recommendation 仍违反可用性目标，catalog 恢复基线估计 0.979900 与 0.12 的网格容差。

Conclusion: 解析式 oracle 与受控实验验证了实现正确性，归档重放确认与两项早期研究的数值连续性，表明工具链具备可靠性与可复现性。

Abstract: Procrustes-Bering-Sheaft automates lightweight model discovery and virtual crash tests for microservices. Each test estimates operation availability by Monte Carlo sampling of service and communication failures. Procrustes checks deployment and tracing evidence; Bering discovers dependencies and incorporates replica declarations; Sheaft evaluates declared operation-success conditions. Independent command-line interfaces exchange reusable, versioned artifacts. The toolchain extends an earlier graph-based approach with mandatory-call contracts, failure-tolerance sweeps, baseline comparison, component-importance analysis, and CI/CD gates. We demonstrate the workflow on one HTTP operation. Six controlled execution states exposed two mismatches between reachability and observed success; an explicit required-call predicate matched all six. Two modelled one-replica additions produced different decisions: recommendation retained an availability-target violation, whereas catalog restored the baseline estimate of 0.979900 and grid tolerance of 0.12. Analytical oracles and controlled tests verify the implementation; archived replay checks numerical continuity with two earlier studies.

</details>


### [262] [CAMGen: A Core Change-Aware Multi-View Framework for Commit Message Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31552)
*Bo Xiong,Weisheng Chen,Zongen Ren,Yifeng Zhu,Xutian Li,Runbang Yan,Chong Wang,Peng Liang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CAMGen：一种核心变更感知的多视图提交信息生成框架，通过功能上下文提升提交信息质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于仓库上下文的LLM提交信息生成方法难以理解局部编辑如何影响由多个代码实体共同实现的功能，而这类功能理解对解释主要变更及其影响至关重要。

Method: CAMGen识别代表主要变更意图的核心实体；通过全仓库语义聚类形成候选功能单元，并选取包含核心实体的单元构建“功能语义视图”以提供受影响功能的语义上下文；同时结合“结构依赖视图”与“历史范例视图”，将三视图上下文与代码差异一同输入LLM指导生成。

Result: 在三种编程语言上优于代表性基线；人工评估显示生成的提交信息更完整，对变更理由与影响解释更清晰。

Conclusion: 功能上下文与互补的仓库信息对提交信息生成具有显著价值。

Abstract: Commit messages help developers understand code changes and support software maintenance. Recent approaches use repository context to improve commit message generation (CMG) with large language models (LLMs). However, existing CMG methods provide limited support for understanding how local edits affect functionality jointly implemented by multiple code entities. This functional understanding is essential for generating commit messages that explain the main change and its impact. To address this problem, we propose CAMGen, a core change-aware multi-view commit message generation framework that incorporates functional context into generation. CAMGen identifies core entities that represent the primary change intent. It then forms candidate functional units through repository-wide semantic clustering and uses those containing the core entities to construct a Functional Semantic View. This view supplies semantic context for interpreting the affected functionality. CAMGen also uses context provided by the Structural Dependency View and the Historical Exemplar View. The context from all three views is then provided to the LLM together with the code diff to guide CMG. The experimental results show that CAMGen outperforms representative CMG baselines across three programming languages. Human evaluation further shows that CAMGen generates more complete commit messages with clearer explanations of change rationale and impact. These results demonstrate the value of functional context and complementary repository information for CMG.

</details>


### [263] [VERA: A Responsible AI Dashboard for Assessing LLM Deployments in Light of the EU AI Act](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31588)
*Robin Quériaux,Solveig Bachellery,Léa Déléris,Saurabhsingh Rajput,Mariam Barry,Tushar Sharma,Jérôme Lebecq*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: VERA是一个开源自托管仪表盘，将COMPL-AI的合规证据可视化，帮助非工程背景的审批人员完成负责任AI评估，将评估时间从56秒降至30秒，可用性评分4.36/5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在受监管场景中部署大语言模型的机构需要负责任AI的技术证据来批准部署。COMPL-AI将欧盟AI法案技术化为6个原则组、18项技术要求（其中12项可由现有基准测量），但产生这些证据的基准套件通常以命令行方式运行，而审批人员多为非工程师，存在使用门槛与流程效率问题。

Method: 提出VERA——开源自托管仪表盘，部署于一家大型欧洲银行。评审者选择模型并运行公开基准；VERA对12项可测量要求打分，并计量每次评估的能耗，增加环境影响层面的运行级证据。用四个已服务的开源权重模型跑通要求流水线。随后开展被试内研究，对比参与者从原始输出文件（基线）与通过VERA仪表盘两种条件下阅读基准结果的表现，并在组织内进行问卷调查。

Result: 仪表盘为每项可测量要求显示分数；被试内研究显示评估所需时间由使用原始文件的平均56秒降至使用仪表盘的30秒；组织内问卷中可用性评分达4.36/5。仪表盘与研究材料已公开以促进可复现性。

Conclusion: 将合规基准证据以面向非工程审批者的仪表盘形式呈现，可显著提升负责任AI评估效率与可用性，并补充能耗等环境影响证据，具有良好的可推广性与可复现性。

Abstract: Institutions that deploy large language models in regulated settings often need technical evidence of responsible AI practices before approving a deployment. COMPL-AI provides a technical interpretation of the EU AI Act that organises this evidence under six principle groups and eighteen technical requirements, twelve of which current benchmarks can measure. The benchmark suites that produce this evidence are typically operated from a command line, while the staff who approve deployments are non-engineers. We present VERA, an open-source self-hosted dashboard deployed to facilitate responsible AI processes within a large European bank. A reviewer selects a model and runs public benchmarks against it. VERA scores the twelve requirements that current benchmarks can measure and instruments the energy consumed by each evaluation, adding run-level evidence for environmental impact. We run four served open-weight models through the requirement pipeline, and the dashboard displays a score for each measurable requirement. A within-subject study then compares how participants read these benchmark results: from the raw output files in one condition, and through the VERA dashboard in the other. The dashboard reduced the time needed to perform the responsible AI assessment, reducing the time required from 56 seconds using raw files to 30 seconds using our dashboard, on average. Our dashboard scored highly (4.36 out of 5) on usefulness in the survey conducted within the organization. The dashboard and the study material are released publicly to promote replicability and reproducibility in different organisational contexts.

</details>
